+    intervening logout. Closing that gap would mean hashing `refreshToken`, which this
+    package explicitly does not do."""
+    doc = _read_credential_json(Path.home() / ".claude/.credentials.json")
+    if doc is None:
+        return ""
+    oauth = doc.get("claudeAiOauth")
+    if not isinstance(oauth, dict):
+        return ""
+    value), hashed before folding into `_cache_key`."""
+    names = ",".join(sorted(pi_auth_provider_keys()))
+    return hashlib.sha256(f"{names}|{PI_PINNED_VERSION}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
 
 
 def _live_opencode_auth_signature(timeout: float) -> str:
     """ADR-0034 (AC-08): a cheap, ALWAYS-fresh (never itself cached) read of `opencode
     auth list --pure` -- credential NAMES only, never a model listing, never persisted
     to disk on its own -- folded into the cache key so the authenticated-provider set is
     genuinely re-checked on every composition that consults the cache, instead of trusted
     from whatever it was when the cache was last written. Any surprise (missing binary,
     timeout, non-zero exit, parse failure) is the empty signature, fail-closed: an
     unreadable auth state simply differs from a real one, so the cache is missed and a
     full fresh probe runs -- it can never fabricate a match."""
     probe_env = dict(os.environ, CI="1", NO_COLOR="1", TERM="dumb")
     try:
         completed = subprocess.run(("opencode", "auth", "list", "--pure"), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                    stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True,
                                    timeout=timeout, check=False, env=probe_env)
     except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
         return ""
     if completed.returncode != 0:
         return ""
     try:
         return ",".join(sorted(_parse_opencode_auth(completed.stdout)))
     except RoutingError:
         return ""
 
 
 def _cache_key(config: dict, auth_signature: str = "") -> str:

-    schema version, always; plus `auth_signature` -- the caller-supplied, freshly-read,
-    normalized authenticated-provider set -- only when the caller actually has one.
+    """ADR-0006 base key (uid + canonical catalog/routing config), the cache schema
+    version, always; extended by ADR-0034 AC-08 with the resolved `opencode` binary's
+    path+mtime, and by ADR-0043 (022 PKG-3, AC-07) with EVERY runtime's own
+    stat/local-read-only credential signature (`_codex_auth_signature`,
+    `_claude_code_auth_signature`, `_pi_auth_signature`) plus the `codex`/`claude`
+    binary signatures (`_binary_signature`, generalized from the opencode-only one) --
+    none of these six calls does any I/O beyond a stat/local read, so this function
+    stays pure/no-subprocess for every existing direct caller (proven by
+    `test_adr0043_ac07_cache_key_never_spawns_a_subprocess_for_the_new_signatures`,
+    tests/test_routing.py). `auth_signature` -- the caller-supplied, freshly-read,
+    normalized opencode-authenticated-provider set, the ONE genuinely subprocess-backed
+    signal in this whole key -- is folded in only when the caller actually has one.
     `probe_inventory` is the only production caller that supplies a non-empty
     `auth_signature` (see `_live_opencode_auth_signature` above); every pre-existing
     direct caller (including tests) keeps calling this with the same two-argument shape
-    it always had and is unaffected."""
+    it always had and is unaffected. The ENTIRE key changes when ANY one runtime's
+    credential state changes -- the probe-cache document covers every runtime's pairs
+    at once, so a credential change in one runtime correctly forces a fresh probe of
+    ALL pairs on the next decision, never a partial/stale mix."""
     canon = json.dumps({"catalog": config.get("catalog", {}), "routing": config.get("routing", {})},
                        sort_keys=True, default=str)
     parts = "\n".join((str(os.getuid()), canon, str(_CACHE_SCHEMA_VERSION),
-                       _opencode_binary_signature(), auth_signature))
+                       _binary_signature("opencode"), _binary_signature("codex"), _binary_signature("claude"),
+                       _codex_auth_signature(), _claude_code_auth_signature(), _pi_auth_signature(),
+                       auth_signature))
     return hashlib.sha256(parts.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
 
 
 def _validate_cache_dir(root: Path) -> bool:
     """Same private-directory discipline as the store: no symlink, this uid's 0700 dir.
 
     Never creates or chmods anything; an unvalidated root just means the cache
     is skipped (read AND write) and every pair is probed fresh (SEC-A03/F06).
     """
     try:
         st = root.lstat()
     except OSError:
         return False
     return (_stat.S_ISDIR(st.st_mode) and not _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode)
             and st.st_uid == os.getuid() and _stat.S_IMODE(st.st_mode) == 0o700)
 
 
 def _read_probe_cache(cache_root, key: str, now: float, config: dict):
     """Valid cache or None; anything unexpected is ignored fail-closed (fresh probes run).
 
     Only PAIRS PRESENT are ever positive (F06: negatives are never persisted,
     so a transient failure costs one retry, never the full TTL). Every entry
-    is re-intersected with the live `_configured_models` catalog on read
-    (F09), so a stale-but-key-matching cache can never widen the audited set.
+    is re-checked against the live `resolve_ceiling` state on read (F09), so a
+    stale-but-key-matching cache can never widen the audited set. ADR-0042 (022 PKG-2):
+    the three tri-states matter here as much as at `_probe_pairs` -- a "veto" provider
+    is dropped outright (never served from cache, however it got written); a "curated"
+    provider is re-intersected exactly like before; an "auto" provider has NO ceiling to
+    intersect against, so the cached models are kept as-is. Naively re-intersecting
+    against `set()` in "auto" mode (the pre-tri-state behavior applied blindly) would
+    leave every auto-mode pair's cached entry EMPTY on every read, defeating the cache
+    entirely -- the caller would re-probe on every single decision (up to
+    `PI_PROBE_MIN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` for pi). See `test_read_probe_cache_auto_mode_is_not_
+    always_empty` in tests/test_routing.py, which exists specifically to catch that
+    regression.
     """
     root = Path(cache_root)
     if not _validate_cache_dir(root):
         return None
     try:
         path = root / "probe-cache.json"
         st = path.lstat()
         if _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not _stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid() or _stat.S_IMODE(st.st_mode) != 0o600:
             return None
         doc = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
         if (not isinstance(doc, dict) or doc.get("key") != key
                 or not isinstance(doc.get("at"), (int, float)) or isinstance(doc.get("at"), bool)
                 or doc["at"] > now or now - doc["at"] > PROBE_CACHE_TTL
                 or not isinstance(doc.get("pairs"), dict)):
             return None
         out: dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]] = {}
         for name, models in doc["pairs"].items():
             runtime, _, provider = str(name).partition("|")
             if (runtime, provider) not in _PAIR_COMMANDS or not isinstance(models, list) or not all(isinstance(m, str) for m in models):
                 return None
-            intersected = set(models) & _configured_models(config, provider)
-            if intersected:  # empty after intersection ⇒ treated as absent, retried like any negative
-                out[(runtime, provider)] = intersected
+            state, ceiling = resolve_ceiling(config, provider)
+            if state == "veto":
+                continue  # never served from cache, no matter what is on disk (AC-05 layer 2)
+            # "auto": no ceiling exists to re-intersect against -- keeping `set()` here
+            # (the naive port of the pre-tri-state one-liner) would empty every auto-mode
+            # entry on every single read, defeating the cache (see docstring above).
+            kept = set(models) if state == "auto" else (set(models) & ceiling)
+            if kept:  # empty after filtering ⇒ treated as absent, retried like any negative
+                out[(runtime, provider)] = kept
         return out
     except (OSError, ValueError):
         return None
 
 
 def _write_probe_cache(cache_root, key: str, result: dict, now: float) -> None:
     """Atomic 0600 tmp+rename under the managed root; content is redacted pair->models only."""
     root = Path(cache_root)
     if not _validate_cache_dir(root):
         return  # the store owns root creation; never create or chmod it from here
     tmp = root / f".probe-cache.{os.getpid()}.tmp"
     doc = {"key": key, "at": now,
            "pairs": {f"{runtime}|{provider}": sorted(models) for (runtime, provider), models in result.items()}}
     old = os.umask(0o077)
     try:
         tmp.write_text(json.dumps(doc, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
         os.chmod(tmp, 0o600)
         os.replace(tmp, root / "probe-cache.json")
     except OSError:
         try: tmp.unlink()
         except OSError: pass
     finally:
         os.umask(old)
 
 
+def prune_legacy_probe_cache(legacy_root) -> bool:
+    """AC-10 (022 PKG-3): a single probe cache lives at the store's cache root now
+    (`RoutingStore.ensure_cache_root()`, `~/.local/state/set-agentes/routing-v2`) --
+    this removes the STALE SIBLING file at the old, divergent location
+    (`STATE_DIR/probe-cache.json`, what `--route-doctor`/`--doctor-all`/the 'Estado
+    general' panel used to read while the decision path already read/wrote the
+    routing-v2 one -- two caches confirmed diverging live, ADR-0043).
+
+    Same security discipline as `_write_probe_cache`, mirrored exactly: the directory
+    must pass `_validate_cache_dir` (0700, this uid, no symlink) and the file itself
+    must independently be a regular, this-uid file (never following a symlink) before
+    it is ever unlinked -- any surprise leaves it untouched (fail-closed: an
+    unrecognized foreign object is never deleted). `STATE_DIR` also holds
+    `config.toml`/`model-preference.toml` and other real state -- this function NEVER
+    touches the directory itself or any other file in it, only this one legacy
+    `probe-cache.json`. Returns True only when a file was actually removed (for the
+    caller's own best-effort bookkeeping); False for "nothing to prune" and every
+    fail-closed branch alike -- the caller never needs to distinguish them, pruning is
+    always best-effort."""
+    root = Path(legacy_root)
+    if not _validate_cache_dir(root):
+        return False
+    path = root / "probe-cache.json"
+    try:
+        st = path.lstat()
+    except OSError:
+        return False
+    if _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not _stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid():
+        return False
+    try:
+        path.unlink()
+    except OSError:
+        return False
+    return True

 def _run_cached(ran, argv, timeout, env):
     """Subprocess run for `argv`, memoized in the caller's `ran` dict (shared across pairs
     in one probe call, e.g. the single `opencode auth list --pure` all opencode pairs
     reuse). Returns the CompletedProcess, or None on any OSError/timeout — never raises."""
     if argv not in ran:
         try:
             ran[argv] = subprocess.run(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                                        text=True, timeout=timeout, check=False, env=env)
         except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
             ran[argv] = None
     return ran[argv]
 
 
 def _probe_pairs(config: dict, pairs, timeout: float, ran=None) -> dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]]:
     """Fresh, uncached, pair-scoped subprocess observation for exactly `pairs`.
 
     Any surprise — missing binary, timeout, nonzero exit, unexpected shape,
     error text — makes only that pair unavailable (never raises). A pair
     without any resulting model is simply absent from the returned dict:
     negative results are never a first-class cached value (F06).
     """
     result: dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]] = {}
     # Identical argvs across pairs (e.g. `opencode auth list` for both opencode pairs) run once.
     ran = {} if ran is None else ran

     # non-TTY stdout unless CI/TERM signal a dumb terminal (observed 2026-07-26).
     probe_env = dict(os.environ, CI="1", NO_COLOR="1", TERM="dumb")
     for pair in pairs:
         runtime, provider = pair
         commands = _PAIR_COMMANDS[pair]

+        # had, so a curated-empty provider still costs nothing to probe. "auto" (no
+        # `[catalog]` key for this provider) used to fall into that SAME branch (an empty
+        # `set()` is falsy too) and get skipped just the same -- exactly the defect this
+        # package removes: `allowed` being `None` below is deliberately NOT treated as
+        # falsy-skip; only genuine credential/parse failure further down drops the pair.
+        state, allowed = resolve_ceiling(config, provider)
+        if state == "veto":
             continue

         # caller's own timeout unchanged.

+                    # ADR-0042 AC-04: codex/claude-code never see state == "auto" here --
+                    # `models_config.load_config` dies if [catalog].codex/.claude are
+                    # absent or empty, so `allowed` is always the curated set by the time

                     # below (an unauthenticated provider simply has no rows in --list-models).
                     if provider not in pi_auth_provider_keys():

+                    # Same guarantee as codex/claude-code above: pi's two pairs are both
+                    # openai-codex/anthropic, always curated by validation.

             except (RoutingError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):

 def probe_inventory(config: dict, timeout: float = 20.0, cache_root=None, fresh: bool = False,
                     pairs=None, now=None, cache_write: bool = True) -> dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]]:
     """Fresh exact pair-scoped observation with per-pair parsers.
 
     Any surprise — missing binary, timeout, nonzero exit, unexpected shape,
     error text — makes only that pair unavailable. Model names are always the
     intersection with the canonical models.toml catalog for the provider, so a
     runtime can never widen the audited model set. Raw provider output
     (including account identifiers) is parsed and discarded, never returned.

     cached (the AM-2 fresh-selected path). Otherwise, with `cache_root`, a

+# AC-05 layer 4 (022 PKG-2): deterministic upper bound on how many NEWLY-synthesized
+# routes a single discovered provider ever contributes to one snapshot, regardless of
+# ceiling state (curated OR auto). Live-measured 2026-08-12: opencode-zen alone lists
+# 58-61 models (`opencode models opencode --pure`), already comfortably under this cap
+# today (curated at 60) -- the cap exists for what today's curated allowlist happens to
+# bound "by accident": an "auto" provider (no `[catalog]` key at all) has NO curated
+# ceiling to keep the raw live-probe count in check, so a provider whose upstream
+# catalog grows past this constant in the future (or is auto from day one) still gets a
+# hard, predictable stop, not silent unbounded growth of the synthesized-route pool that
+# feeds `service.route`'s candidate sort. Alphabetical truncation (`sorted()`, never
+# probe/dict iteration order) keeps which models survive the cut IDENTICAL across runs
+# for the same inputs -- "deterministic" is about repeatability, not about the cut being
+# the "best" N by any other measure. Applied to the post-filter (curated/excluded ids
+# already dropped) candidate list per provider, so the cap bounds actual pool growth,
+# not merely how many raw ids were considered.
+_DISCOVERED_ROUTE_CAP_PER_PROVIDER = 80
+
+
 def discovered_models(config: dict, provider: str, inventory: dict) -> set[str]:
     """Every model the probe actually observed for `provider`, across runtimes.
 
-    Unlike `_configured_models` (curated ceiling, intersection semantics), this is
-    the raw observed pool — the [catalog] allowlist already shaped what the probe
-    could return for the two OpenCode-lane providers, and the synthesized-route
-    layer applies its own exclusions on top.
+    Unlike `resolve_ceiling` (curated ceiling, tri-state), this is the raw observed
+    pool — the [catalog] allowlist already shaped what the probe could return for a
+    "curated" provider (never for "auto", by definition), and the synthesized-route
+    layer applies its own exclusions and cap on top.
     """
     pool: set[str] = set()
     for (_, prov), models in inventory.items():
         if prov == provider:
             pool |= {m for m in models if isinstance(m, str) and m}
     return pool
 
 
 def build_effective_snapshot(catalog_path: Path, roster: list[dict], config: dict,
                              inventory: dict, digest=None):
     """Curated snapshot ∪ synthesized routes for discovered, uncurated models.
 
     Returns `(CatalogSnapshot, frozenset[str])` — the second element is the set of
     route_ids whose tier/family are INFERRED (routing_core/inference.py), so the
     decision layer can mark them `MODEL_METADATA_INFERRED` and apply the
     inference-only-removes-independence rule. Precedence rules (ADR-0029 d.2):
     a curated row with the same (provider, canonical_model) wins — no synthesized
     twin is ever added; `[catalog].exclude` entries (`"provider:model"` strings in
-    models.toml) veto individual discovered ids; providers outside the audited
-    `_PAIR_COMMANDS` set are never synthesized, no matter what the config says.
+    models.toml) veto individual discovered ids, and (ADR-0042, 022 PKG-2, AC-05 layer
+    4) a `"provider:*"` entry vetoes the ENTIRE provider from this synthesized path in
+    one line, without touching `[routing].discovered_providers` or `resolve_ceiling`'s
+    own veto (a different axis: this is "never synthesize", not "never probe"); providers
+    outside the audited `_PAIR_COMMANDS` set are never synthesized, no matter what the
+    config says; and `_DISCOVERED_ROUTE_CAP_PER_PROVIDER` bounds how many NEW routes one
+    provider ever contributes (AC-05 layer 4's other half).
 
     ADR-0034: `providers` comes from `resolve_discovered_providers` -- the single
     function that also drives `service.py`'s recheck lambda, so `"auto"` derives from
     the SAME live-inventory-intersect-audited rule both places (AC-02).
     """
     from .inference import synthesize_route_row
     base = build_snapshot(catalog_path, roster, config, digest)
     providers = resolve_discovered_providers(config, inventory)
     if not providers:
         return base, frozenset()
     roster_names = tuple(sorted({row["role"] for row in roster}))
     curated_ids = {(r.provider, canonical_model(r.provider, r.model)) for r in base.routes}
     exclusions = set()
     for item in (config.get("catalog") or {}).get("exclude") or []:
         if isinstance(item, str) and ":" in item:
             exclusions.add(tuple(item.split(":", 1)))
     audited: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
     for runtime, prov in _PAIR_COMMANDS:
         audited.setdefault(prov, set()).add(runtime)
     routes = list(base.routes)
     identities = set(base.identities)
     inferred: set[str] = set()
     for provider in providers:
-        if provider not in audited:
+        if provider not in audited or (provider, "*") in exclusions:
             continue
-        for model in sorted(discovered_models(config, provider, inventory)):
-            if (provider, canonical_model(provider, model)) in curated_ids:
-                continue
-            if (provider, model) in exclusions:
-                continue
+        candidates = [model for model in sorted(discovered_models(config, provider, inventory))
+                     if (provider, canonical_model(provider, model)) not in curated_ids
+                     and (provider, model) not in exclusions]
+        for model in candidates[:_DISCOVERED_ROUTE_CAP_PER_PROVIDER]:
             row = synthesize_route_row(provider, model, roster_names)
             roles = tuple(sorted(row["roles"]))
             tools = tuple(sorted(row["tools"]))
             rid = StaticRoute.identifier(2, provider, model, row["family"], row["effort"],
                                          (row["tier"],), roles, tools, row["curated_priority"])
             route = StaticRoute(2, provider, model, row["family"], row["effort"], row["tier"],
                                 roles, tools, row["curated_priority"], rid)
             routes.append(route)
             inferred.add(rid)
             for runtime in audited[provider]:
                 if model in inventory.get((runtime, provider), set()):
                     identities.add((rid, runtime, provider, model, row["family"], row["effort"]))
     return CatalogSnapshot(tuple(routes), frozenset(identities)), frozenset(inferred)

    95	# --------------------------------------------------- 014-model-preference-policy (AC-02)
    96	#
    97	# One real, per-harness-install sibling config file next to `APP_CONFIG` (the same
    98	# private `STATE_DIR`, `write_app_config`'s own directory) -- NEVER routed through
    99	# `write_app_config` (its flat `key = value` serializer, `:XXX` below, corrupts a nested
   100	# preference table) or `app_config()` (its silent parse-failure-as-`{}` swallow). This
   101	# file gets its OWN dedicated, atomic, fail-closed round-trip: `atomic_write` (already
   102	# established for the Claude settings JSON writer above) for the temp-file+`os.replace`
   103	# discipline, and a small, purpose-built two-table TOML serializer for a schema this
   104	# contract fully owns and closes (`[preference]`, `[role_override]`) -- not a
   105	# general-purpose nested-TOML writer (round-2 finding R2-F-04).
   155	    convenience, never a requirement for pins/preferences to keep working.
   156	
   157	    F-05 (P1 repair): the union only ever has an observable effect on a day
   158	    `resolve_discovered_providers` can return a provider OUTSIDE the base set --
   159	    which today it structurally cannot: every provider it can produce comes from
   160	    `routing_core.catalog._PAIR_COMMANDS`'s own audited provider set, and that set is
   161	    pinned equal to `models_config.DISCOVERABLE_PROVIDERS` (== `_MODEL_PREFERENCE_
   162	    PROVIDERS`, guarded for real since ADR-0042 -- both are derived from
   163	    `provider_registry.PROVIDERS`, and `test_adr0042_ac01b_...` in tests/test_routing.py
   164	    compares this tuple against `_PAIR_COMMANDS`'s own provider set directly, never a
   165	    second hardcoded literal). So the probe below is skipped whenever the
   166	    base set already covers `DISCOVERABLE_PROVIDERS` -- a cheap, exact short-circuit
   167	    that avoids paying a subprocess probe (each `_PAIR_COMMANDS` entry can block up to
   168	    20s) on every `--model-pin-set`/`--model-preference-set` call for a union that
   169	    cannot widen anything yet. The probe only actually runs again once
   170	    `_PAIR_COMMANDS`'s audited set genuinely grows past the base set (e.g. a future
   171	    ADR widens discovery beyond today's four providers)."""
   172	    if set(models_config.DISCOVERABLE_PROVIDERS) <= set(_MODEL_PREFERENCE_PROVIDERS):
   173	        return _MODEL_PREFERENCE_PROVIDERS
   174	    try:
   175	        from routing_core.catalog import probe_inventory, resolve_discovered_providers
   176	        config = models_config.load_config()
   177	        # ADR-0043 (022 PKG-3, AC-10): the single store root, not the legacy STATE_DIR.
   178	        inventory = probe_inventory(config, cache_root=_probe_cache_root())
   179	        live = resolve_discovered_providers(config, inventory)
   180	    except Exception:
   181	        return _MODEL_PREFERENCE_PROVIDERS
   182	    return tuple(dict.fromkeys((*_MODEL_PREFERENCE_PROVIDERS, *live)))

  3162	def _estado_general_lines(data):
  3163	    """The '🩺 Estado general' panel: everything --doctor-all knows, formatted for
  3164	    humans — harnesses with version+auth, install scope, catalog CLIs, live
  3165	    providers. Reuses _status_data's rows (already probed) and the shared probe
  3166	    cache; never prints credential material.
  3167	
  3168	    ADR-0043 (022 PKG-3, AC-10): this is the first-menu-item 'vidriera' surface —
  3169	    probes against the SAME cache root `--route-decide` uses (`_probe_cache_root()`),
  3170	    never the legacy `STATE_DIR` one, and best-effort prunes the stale legacy sibling
  3171	    on the way (same discipline as `_write_probe_cache`)."""
  3172	    lines = ["Harnesses"]
  3173	    lines += table_lines([(cli, version, auth) for cli, version, auth in data["rows"]])
  3174	    lines += table_lines([("pi", {"yes": "instalado", "via-pnpm-dlx": "vía pnpm dlx",
  3175	                                  "no": "FALTA (sin pnpm)"}[_pi_lane_state()], "-")])
  3176	    scope = _install_scope()
  3177	    if isinstance(scope, list):
  3178	        lines += ["", "Alcance de instalación", *table_lines([(", ".join(scope) or "none",)])]
  3179	    lines.append("")
  3180	    lines.append("Herramientas (catálogo)")
  3181	    lines += table_lines([(name, "instalado" if installed else "falta") for name, installed in _tools_data()])
  3182	    lines.append("")
  3183	    lines.append("Proveedores autenticados (probe)")
  3184	    try:
  3185	        from routing_core.catalog import prune_legacy_probe_cache, probe_inventory
  3186	        prune_legacy_probe_cache(STATE_DIR)
  3187	        inventory = probe_inventory(models_config.load_config(), cache_root=_probe_cache_root())
  3188	        pairs = [(provider, runtime, f"{len(models)} modelos")
  3189	                 for (runtime, provider), models in sorted(inventory.items()) if models]
  3190	        lines += table_lines(pairs) if pairs else ["  (ninguno — logueate en al menos una herramienta)"]
  3191	    except Exception:
  3192	        lines.append("  probe no disponible ahora (corré set-agents --doctor-all)")
  3193	    if data["drift"] == "stale":
  3194	        lines += ["", "drift: la instalación quedó atrás del repo → Instalar / Reparar o ./build.sh --install"]
  3195	    return lines

   271	def _probe_cache_root() -> Path:
   272	    """ADR-0043 (022 PKG-3, AC-10): the single root `RoutingService`'s own composition
   273	    creates/validates via `RoutingStore.ensure_cache_root()` (routing_core/service.py)
   274	    -- never the second, `STATE_DIR`-shaped literal this used to duplicate. Lazy import
   275	    (same "no routing_core dependency at module load" discipline the two callers below
   276	    already document). `RoutingStore.root` is a pure attribute (no I/O at
   277	    construction), so this costs nothing beyond the `Path.home() / ...` literal it
   278	    replaces; the private-directory validation (and any creation) stays inside

   284	def detect_subscriptions(config):
   285	    """Probe-backed subscription detection (ADR-0029): which subscriptions this
   286	    machine can actually use, derived from authenticated pairs. Lazy import (no
   287	    routing_core dependency at module load), shared probe cache, and `None` on
   288	    any failure — the caller treats that as "no probe info", never as "nothing".
   289	    Never reads or returns credential material."""
   290	    try:
   291	        from routing_core.catalog import probe_inventory
   292	        inventory = probe_inventory(config, cache_root=_probe_cache_root())
   293	    except Exception:
   294	        return None
   295	    return {
   296	        _PROVIDER_SUBSCRIPTION[provider]
   297	        for (_, provider), models in inventory.items()
   298	        if models and provider in _PROVIDER_SUBSCRIPTION
   299	    }

   301	
   302	def auto_profile(config=None):
   303	    """The lane is derived from the probe, never hand-picked (the old use-*.sh
   304	    scripts are gone). Both opencode pairs live → go-zen; only opencode-zen →
   305	    zen; probe fine but no opencode pair → local. None on probe failure — the
   306	    caller keeps whatever active-profile already says and never dies."""
   307	    try:
   308	        from routing_core.catalog import probe_inventory
   309	        cfg = config if config is not None else load_config()
   310	        inventory = probe_inventory(cfg, cache_root=_probe_cache_root())
   311	    except Exception:
   312	        return None
   313	    live = {provider for (_, provider), models in inventory.items() if models}
   314	    if "opencode-go" in live:
   315	        return "go-zen"
   316	    if "opencode-zen" in live:
   317	        return "zen"
   318	    return "local"

   321	def subscription_of(model, config):
   322	    prefix = model.split("/", 1)[0]
   323	    providers = {**SUBSCRIPTION_BY_PREFIX, **config["providers"]}
   324	    if prefix not in providers:
   325	        die(f"unknown provider prefix '{prefix}' in {model} — add it to [providers] in models.toml")

 #!/usr/bin/env python3
 """Model routing source of truth: load, validate, and emit models.toml + roles.tsv.
 
 roles.tsv holds structure only (role, mode, temperature, capability, duty);
 models.toml declares subscriptions, the model catalog, and the model assigned
 to each area (duty), with optional per-role overrides. The go-zen/zen/local
 profiles survive as lanes of the opencode dimension.
 """
 
 import csv
 import json
 import re
 import sys
 import tomllib
 import os
 import tempfile
 from pathlib import Path
 
+# ADR-0042 (022 PKG-1): the single provider registry. Deliberately NOT a `routing_core`
+# import -- `provider_registry.py` has zero dependencies of its own (no `routing_core`, no
+# `models_config`), so importing it here at module scope costs nothing, unlike
+# `routing_core.catalog` (see `detect_subscriptions`/`auto_profile` below, both of which
+# still lazily import that ONE module for a real reason: any `routing_core` submodule
+# import runs `routing_core/__init__.py`, which loads `service.py`/`store.py` -- sqlite3,
+# subprocess -- at import time).
+import provider_registry

 def detect_subscriptions(config):
     """Probe-backed subscription detection (ADR-0029): which subscriptions this
     machine can actually use, derived from authenticated pairs. Lazy import (no
     routing_core dependency at module load), shared probe cache, and `None` on
     any failure — the caller treats that as "no probe info", never as "nothing".

     except Exception:
         return None
     return {
         _PROVIDER_SUBSCRIPTION[provider]
         for (_, provider), models in inventory.items()
         if models and provider in _PROVIDER_SUBSCRIPTION
     }
 
 
 def auto_profile(config=None):
     """The lane is derived from the probe, never hand-picked (the old use-*.sh
     scripts are gone). Both opencode pairs live → go-zen; only opencode-zen →
     zen; probe fine but no opencode pair → local. None on probe failure — the
     caller keeps whatever active-profile already says and never dies."""
     try:
         from routing_core.catalog import probe_inventory
         cfg = config if config is not None else load_config()
-        state = Path.home() / ".local/state/set-agentes"
-        inventory = probe_inventory(cfg, cache_root=state)
+        inventory = probe_inventory(cfg, cache_root=_probe_cache_root())
     except Exception:
         return None
     live = {provider for (_, provider), models in inventory.items() if models}
     if "opencode-go" in live:
         return "go-zen"
     if "opencode-zen" in live:
         return "zen"
     return "local"
 
 
 def subscription_of(model, config):
     prefix = model.split("/", 1)[0]
     providers = {**SUBSCRIPTION_BY_PREFIX, **config["providers"]}
     if prefix not in providers:
         die(f"unknown provider prefix '{prefix}' in {model} — add it to [providers] in models.toml")
     return providers[prefix]

 def _effective_preference_providers():

+    `provider_registry.PROVIDERS` registry since ADR-0042; the two differ in TYPE on
+    purpose, ordered tuple vs. unordered set, never in membership) UNION whatever the

     resolvable -- a live probe, never network/credential material. Deliberately never
     called from `load_model_preference`/`load_model_pin` (the boot path, `_read_model_
     preference_raw`'s callers): probing there would make a plain `models.toml` read
     depend on a live opencode probe, which the spec explicitly forbids. Only the WRITE
     CLI paths below (`cmd_model_preference_set`, `cmd_model_pin_set`, ...) -- explicit,
     interactive user actions where the probe's cost is acceptable -- use this. ANY
     failure (missing routing_core state, a broken probe, an unreadable models.toml)
     degrades silently to the base set alone, never a crash: a wider live set is a
     convenience, never a requirement for pins/preferences to keep working.

     `resolve_discovered_providers` can return a provider OUTSIDE the base set --

+    `provider_registry.PROVIDERS`, and `test_adr0042_ac01b_...` in tests/test_routing.py
+    compares this tuple against `_PAIR_COMMANDS`'s own provider set directly, never a

 def _validate_preference_providers(class_name, providers, valid_providers=_MODEL_PREFERENCE_PROVIDERS):
     """AC-02 resolution states 1/2/4d: shared by the config-load path and the CLI write
     path (`load_model_preference`/`cmd_model_preference_set` below) -- a malformed value

         if not isinstance(token, str) or token not in valid_providers:
             _model_preference_die(f"model-preference.toml: [preference].{class_name} contains unknown provider {token!r}")
         validated.append(token)

 def use_color():
     return sys.stdout.isatty() and not os.environ.get("NO_COLOR") and os.environ.get("TERM") != "dumb"
 
 
 def color(text, code):

@@ -3098,56 +3146,62 @@ def table_lines(rows, indent="  "):
     renderer for every menu panel (Estado, Modelos header, Herramientas). Pure on
     its inputs except for the width read; no hardcoded widths, no ANSI inside
     (escape codes break the length math), colors get applied by callers on whole lines."""
     rows = [tuple(str(cell) for cell in row) for row in rows]
     if not rows:
         return []
     columns = max(len(row) for row in rows)
     widths = [max((len(row[i]) for row in rows if len(row) > i), default=0) for i in range(columns)]
     width = _term_width()
     lines = []
     for row in rows:
         cells = [row[i].ljust(widths[i]) for i in range(len(row))]
         lines.append(_clip((indent + "  ".join(cells)).rstrip(), width))
     return lines

   600	def _write_probe_cache(cache_root, key: str, result: dict, now: float) -> None:
   601	    """Atomic 0600 tmp+rename under the managed root; content is redacted pair->models only."""
   602	    root = Path(cache_root)
   603	    if not _validate_cache_dir(root):
   604	        return  # the store owns root creation; never create or chmod it from here
   605	    tmp = root / f".probe-cache.{os.getpid()}.tmp"
   606	    doc = {"key": key, "at": now,
   607	           "pairs": {f"{runtime}|{provider}": sorted(models) for (runtime, provider), models in result.items()}}
   608	    old = os.umask(0o077)
   609	    try:
   610	        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(doc, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
   611	        os.chmod(tmp, 0o600)
   612	        os.replace(tmp, root / "probe-cache.json")
   613	    except OSError:
   614	        try: tmp.unlink()
   615	        except OSError: pass
   616	    finally:
   617	        os.umask(old)

   620	def prune_legacy_probe_cache(legacy_root) -> bool:
   621	    """AC-10 (022 PKG-3): a single probe cache lives at the store's cache root now
   622	    (`RoutingStore.ensure_cache_root()`, `~/.local/state/set-agentes/routing-v2`) --
   623	    this removes the STALE SIBLING file at the old, divergent location
   624	    (`STATE_DIR/probe-cache.json`, what `--route-doctor`/`--doctor-all`/the 'Estado
   625	    general' panel used to read while the decision path already read/wrote the
   626	    routing-v2 one -- two caches confirmed diverging live, ADR-0043).
   627	
   628	    Same security discipline as `_write_probe_cache`, mirrored exactly: the directory
   629	    must pass `_validate_cache_dir` (0700, this uid, no symlink) and the file itself
   630	    must independently be a regular, this-uid file (never following a symlink) before
   631	    it is ever unlinked -- any surprise leaves it untouched (fail-closed: an
   632	    unrecognized foreign object is never deleted). `STATE_DIR` also holds
   633	    `config.toml`/`model-preference.toml` and other real state -- this function NEVER
   634	    touches the directory itself or any other file in it, only this one legacy
   635	    `probe-cache.json`. Returns True only when a file was actually removed (for the
   636	    caller's own best-effort bookkeeping); False for "nothing to prune" and every
   637	    fail-closed branch alike -- the caller never needs to distinguish them, pruning is
   638	    always best-effort."""
   639	    root = Path(legacy_root)
   640	    if not _validate_cache_dir(root):
   641	        return False
   642	    path = root / "probe-cache.json"
   643	    try:
   644	        st = path.lstat()
   645	    except OSError:
   646	        return False
   647	    if _stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not _stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) or st.st_uid != os.getuid():
   648	        return False
   649	    try:
   650	        path.unlink()
   651	    except OSError:
   652	        return False
   653	    return True
   654

   656	def _run_cached(ran, argv, timeout, env):
   657	    """Subprocess run for `argv`, memoized in the caller's `ran` dict (shared across pairs
   658	    in one probe call, e.g. the single `opencode auth list --pure` all opencode pairs
   659	    reuse). Returns the CompletedProcess, or None on any OSError/timeout — never raises."""
   660	    if argv not in ran:
   661	        try:
   662	            ran[argv] = subprocess.run(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
   663	                                       text=True, timeout=timeout, check=False, env=env)
   664	        except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   665	            ran[argv] = None
   666	    return ran[argv]
   667

   669	def _probe_pairs(config: dict, pairs, timeout: float, ran=None) -> dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]]:
   670	    """Fresh, uncached, pair-scoped subprocess observation for exactly `pairs`.
   671	
   672	    Any surprise — missing binary, timeout, nonzero exit, unexpected shape,
   673	    error text — makes only that pair unavailable (never raises). A pair
   674	    without any resulting model is simply absent from the returned dict:
   675	    negative results are never a first-class cached value (F06).
   676	    """
   677	    result: dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]] = {}
   678	    # Identical argvs across pairs (e.g. `opencode auth list` for both opencode pairs) run once.
   679	    ran = {} if ran is None else ran
   680	    # Non-interactive probe environment: opencode's TUI writer blocks forever on a
   681	    # non-TTY stdout unless CI/TERM signal a dumb terminal (observed 2026-07-26).
   682	    probe_env = dict(os.environ, CI="1", NO_COLOR="1", TERM="dumb")
   683	    for pair in pairs:
   684	        runtime, provider = pair
   685	        commands = _PAIR_COMMANDS[pair]
   686	        # ADR-0042 (022 PKG-2, AC-04): "veto" is dropped before any subprocess runs at
   687	        # all -- same early-exit position the pre-tri-state `if not allowed: continue`
   688	        # had, so a curated-empty provider still costs nothing to probe. "auto" (no
   689	        # `[catalog]` key for this provider) used to fall into that SAME branch (an empty
   690	        # `set()` is falsy too) and get skipped just the same -- exactly the defect this
   691	        # package removes: `allowed` being `None` below is deliberately NOT treated as
   692	        # falsy-skip; only genuine credential/parse failure further down drops the pair.
   693	        state, allowed = resolve_ceiling(config, provider)
   694	        if state == "veto":
   695	            continue
   696	        # PKG-N02: only the pi pairs get the cold-pnpm floor; every other pair keeps the
   697	        # caller's own timeout unchanged.
   698	        pair_timeout = max(timeout, PI_PROBE_MIN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS) if runtime == "pi" else timeout
   699	        if runtime == "opencode":
   700	            # 012 AC-02: the credential-set check (map 1) and the models-listing CLI
   701	            # id/prefix (map 2) are two independently addressable lookups, never one.
   702	            # Repair F-06 (012 repair, medium, +69%/+10.5s measured without this fix):
   703	            # map 1's membership check runs BEFORE the second, more expensive `opencode
   704	            # models <id>` call, not after both commands already ran — a machine missing
   705	            # a given OpenCode-lane subscription now never pays that pair's models-call
   706	            # latency at all, instead of always paying it only to discard the result.
   707	            auth_result = _run_cached(ran, commands[0], pair_timeout, probe_env)
   708	            if auth_result is None or auth_result.returncode != 0:
   709	                continue
   710	            try:
   711	                provider_key = _OPENCODE_PROVIDER_KEYS[provider]
   712	                credentials = _parse_opencode_auth(auth_result.stdout)
   713	            except (RoutingError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
   714	                continue
   715	            if provider_key not in credentials:
   716	                continue
   717	            models_result = _run_cached(ran, commands[1], pair_timeout, probe_env)
   718	            if models_result is None or models_result.returncode != 0:
   719	                continue
   720	            try:
   721	                cli_id = _OPENCODE_CLI_IDS[provider]
   722	                parsed = _parse_opencode_models(models_result.stdout, cli_id)
   723	            except (RoutingError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
   724	                continue
   725	            # AC-05 layer 3 is unaffected by "auto": billing_rank's fail-closed-to-
   726	            # expensive default already applies to any provider PROVIDER_BILLING_KIND
   727	            # does not classify as "subscription" -- ceiling state never enters that map.
   728	            models = parsed if state == "auto" else (allowed & parsed)
   729	        else:
   730	            completed, failed = [], False
   731	            for argv in commands:
   732	                completed_item = _run_cached(ran, argv, pair_timeout, probe_env)
   733	                if completed_item is None:
   734	                    failed = True; break
   735	                completed.append(completed_item)
   736	            if failed or any(item.returncode != 0 for item in completed):
   737	                continue
   738	            try:
   739	                if runtime == "codex":
   740	                    # ADR-0042 AC-04: codex/claude-code never see state == "auto" here --
   741	                    # `models_config.load_config` dies if [catalog].codex/.claude are
   742	                    # absent or empty, so `allowed` is always the curated set by the time
   743	                    # a validated config reaches this function (see resolve_ceiling's
   744	                    # own docstring). Neither runtime has a models-LISTING command to
   745	                    # fall back on if it ever were "auto" (only a login-status boolean).
   745	                    # fall back on if it ever were "auto" (only a login-status boolean).
   746	                    models = allowed if _parse_codex_login(completed[0].stdout, completed[0].stderr) else set()
   747	                elif runtime == "claude-code":
   748	                    models = allowed if _parse_claude_auth(completed[0].stdout) else set()
   749	                else:  # pi
   750	                    # Belt-and-suspenders (T-305, spike Q2): the auth.json key-set is a cheap,
   751	                    # non-subprocess signal alongside the naturally fail-closed column-parse
   752	                    # below (an unauthenticated provider simply has no rows in --list-models).
   753	                    if provider not in pi_auth_provider_keys():
   754	                        continue
   755	                    # Same guarantee as codex/claude-code above: pi's two pairs are both
   756	                    # openai-codex/anthropic, always curated by validation.
   757	                    models = allowed & _parse_pi_models(completed[0].stdout, provider)
   758	            except (RoutingError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
   759	                continue
   760	        if models:
   761	            result[pair] = models
   762	    return result

   765	def probe_inventory(config: dict, timeout: float = 20.0, cache_root=None, fresh: bool = False,
   766	                    pairs=None, now=None, cache_write: bool = True) -> dict[tuple[str, str], set[str]]:
   767	    """Fresh exact pair-scoped observation with per-pair parsers.
   768	
   769	    Any surprise — missing binary, timeout, nonzero exit, unexpected shape,
   770	    error text — makes only that pair unavailable. Model names are always the
   771	    intersection with the canonical models.toml catalog for the provider, so a
   772	    runtime can never widen the audited model set. Raw provider output
   773	    (including account identifiers) is parsed and discarded, never returned.
   774	
   775	    `pairs` restricts probing to those exact pairs, always fresh and never
   776	    cached (the AM-2 fresh-selected path). Otherwise, with `cache_root`, a
   777	    valid unexpired cache (ADR-0006 key) filters candidates; pairs ABSENT
   778	    from that cache (never-seen or previously negative, F06) are always
   779	    re-probed fresh regardless of TTL, so a transient failure only ever
   780	    costs one retry, never the whole cache window. `cache_write=False`
   781	    (SEC-A03: the simulate/explain lane) reads a warm cache but never
   782	    persists one, preserving explain's no-mutation contract.
   783	
   784	    ADR-0034 (AC-08): whenever a cache is even consulted, the authenticated-opencode-
   785	    provider set is re-read fresh (`_live_opencode_auth_signature`, one cheap credential-
   786	    only call, never itself cached) and folded into the lookup/write key -- a credential
   787	    change is caught immediately, never only after the TTL window; the cache on disk
   788	    still holds model LISTINGS only, never the raw signature.
   789	    """
   790	    now = time.time() if now is None else now
   791	    if pairs is not None:
   792	        selected = [pair for pair in pairs if pair in _PAIR_COMMANDS]
   793	        return _probe_pairs(config, selected, timeout)
   794	    all_pairs = list(_PAIR_COMMANDS)
   795	    use_cache = cache_root is not None and _validate_cache_dir(Path(cache_root))
   796	    auth_signature = _live_opencode_auth_signature(timeout) if use_cache else ""
   797	    key = _cache_key(config, auth_signature) if use_cache else None
   798	    cached = _read_probe_cache(cache_root, key, now, config) if (use_cache and not fresh) else None
   799	    if cached is not None:
   800	        missing = [pair for pair in all_pairs if pair not in cached]
   801	        if not missing:
   802	            return cached
   803	        merged = dict(cached)
   804	        merged.update(_probe_pairs(config, missing, timeout))
   805	    else:
   806	        merged = _probe_pairs(config, all_pairs, timeout)
   807	    if use_cache and cache_write:
   808	        _write_probe_cache(cache_root, key, merged, now)
   809	    return merged
   810	
   811	
   812	def _check_family_collisions(rows) -> None:
   813	    """012 AC-07: `family` stays fully curated, never vendor-captured — but for any model
   814	    id curated under more than one provider, every curated row for that id must share the
   815	    identical (curator-normalized) `family` value, so REVIEW_FAMILY_CONFLICT/
   816	    REVIEW_PROVIDER_CONFLICT (service.py) can never be satisfied independently by the same
   817	    underlying model reviewing itself under two different provider names (measured live:
   818	    `minimax-m2.7` reports two DIFFERENT vendor `family` strings under `opencode` vs
   819	    `opencode-go` — a literal vendor copy would fabricate false reviewer independence for
   820	    that id; see docs/specs/012-discovered-inventory/spec.md AC-07).
   821	
   822	    Repair SEC-001 (panel RP-01, critical): the row key is `canonical_model(provider,
   823	    model)`, never the raw `model` string. A raw-string key only ever catches the SAME id
   824	    curated under two providers; it is blind to the SAME underlying model curated under
   825	    two DIFFERENT ids (e.g. `anthropic`/`opus` and `opencode-zen`/`claude-opus-4-8`,
   826	    proven identical in-repo by PI_MODEL_MAP) — exactly the gap the panel's PoC exploited.
   827	    `row.get("provider")` falls back to the model id itself when a row carries no
   828	    `provider` key (kept for the pre-existing pure-function unit tests that call this
   829	    directly with `{"model": ..., "family": ...}` fixtures only), so canonicalization is
   830	    strictly additive and never changes behavior for a provider-less caller.
   831	
   832	    Pure function over any row sequence exposing `model`/`family` (plain dicts, including
   833	    synthetic fixtures, or the same row shape build_snapshot already validates) — needs no
   834	    probe, no `--verbose`, and no curated routes.v1.toml rows for the new providers to be
   835	    unit-tested today. Raises RoutingError("CATALOG_FAMILY_COLLISION") on the first
   836	    collision found (repair F-12: differentiated from the generic "CATALOG_INVALID" every
   837	    other build_snapshot failure raises, same style already used to tell CATALOG_COLLISION
   838	    apart from CATALOG_INVALID below) — never silently accepts one.
   839	    """
   840	    seen: dict[str, str] = {}
   841	    for row in rows:
   842	        model, family = row["model"], row["family"]
   843	        key = canonical_model(row["provider"], model) if row.get("provider") is not None else model
   844	        if key in seen and seen[key] != family:
   845	            raise RoutingError("CATALOG_FAMILY_COLLISION")
   846	        seen[key] = family
   847

   854	        rows = source["routes"]
   855	        enabled = set(config["routing"]["enabled_providers"])

  3882	            svc = routing.RoutingService._for_tests(snapshot, self.roster, inventory, routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(Path(td) / "state"))
  3883	            request = routing.TaskRequest("implementer", "change", "documentation", selected_runtime="opencode")
  3884	            decision = svc.route(request, self.observed(svc, "implementer", "opencode"))

  3886	        self.assertEqual(decision.reason_codes, ("AUTHORIZATION_INVALID",))

  3890	    def test_ac11_cache_key_covers_the_new_allowlists_and_negatives_stay_unpersisted(self):
  3891	        """Repair F-07 (012 repair, medium): AC-11's actual content -- `_cache_key` hashes
  3892	        `config['catalog']` wholesale, so a cache written before this package's
  3893	        [catalog].opencode_zen/opencode_go keys existed (or with a narrower allowlist)
  3894	        can never silently claim a wider Zen/Go pair; and the "negatives are never
  3895	        persisted" rule (F06) applies unchanged to the two new pairs, not just the two
  3896	        pre-existing ones -- neither half was previously exercised by any test."""
  3897	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  3898	        before = json.loads(json.dumps(self.config))
  3899	        del before["catalog"]["opencode_zen"]; del before["catalog"]["opencode_go"]
  3900	        self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(before), cat._cache_key(self.config))
  3901	        narrower = json.loads(json.dumps(self.config))
  3902	        narrower["catalog"]["opencode_zen"] = narrower["catalog"]["opencode_zen"][:1]
  3903	        self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(narrower), cat._cache_key(self.config))
  3904	
  3905	        def fake_run(argv, **kwargs):
  3906	            if argv[1:4] == ("auth", "list", "--pure"):
  3907	                return types.SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout="●  OpenCode Go api\n", stderr="")
  3908	            return types.SimpleNamespace(returncode=1, stdout="", stderr="Error: Provider not found\n")
  3909	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  3910	            cache_root = Path(td) / "root"; cache_root.mkdir(mode=0o700)
  3911	            with mock.patch.object(cat.subprocess, "run", side_effect=fake_run):
  3912	                first = cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=cache_root, now=1000.0)
  3913	            self.assertNotIn(("opencode", "opencode-zen"), first)  # negative: credential absent
  3914	            self.assertNotIn(("opencode", "opencode-go"), first)  # negative: models call fails (rc=1)
  3915	            cache_doc = json.loads((cache_root / "probe-cache.json").read_text())
  3916	            self.assertNotIn("opencode|opencode-zen", cache_doc["pairs"])
  3917	            self.assertNotIn("opencode|opencode-go", cache_doc["pairs"])

  3921	    def test_adr0043_ac07_ac08_codex_auth_signature_ignores_rotating_material_reacts_to_logout(self):
  3922	        """AC-07 (stat-only, no subprocess) + AC-08's two properties for codex: a refresh
  3923	        (rotates last_refresh/every token field, keeps tokens.account_id and the
  3924	        OPENAI_API_KEY presence bit) never moves the signature; a logout (file deleted)
  3925	        moves it immediately, never a fabricated match with the empty sentinel."""
  3926	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  3927	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  3928	            home = Path(td); codex_dir = home / ".codex"; codex_dir.mkdir()
  3929	            auth = codex_dir / "auth.json"
  3930	            auth.write_text(json.dumps({
  3931	                "OPENAI_API_KEY": None, "auth_mode": "chatgpt", "last_refresh": "2026-08-12T00:00:00Z",
  3932	                "tokens": {"access_token": "secret-before", "account_id": "acct_123",
  3933	                           "id_token": "id-before", "refresh_token": "refresh-before"},
  3934	            }))
  3935	            with mock.patch.object(cat.Path, "home", return_value=home):
  3936	                before = cat._codex_auth_signature()
  3937	                self.assertTrue(before)
  3938	                self.assertNotIn("acct_123", before); self.assertNotIn("secret-before", before)
  3939	                # AC-08 property 1: refresh.
  3940	                auth.write_text(json.dumps({
  3941	                    "OPENAI_API_KEY": None, "auth_mode": "chatgpt", "last_refresh": "2026-08-12T00:05:00Z",
  3942	                    "tokens": {"access_token": "secret-AFTER", "account_id": "acct_123",
  3943	                               "id_token": "id-AFTER", "refresh_token": "refresh-AFTER"},
  3944	                }))
  3945	                self.assertEqual(cat._codex_auth_signature(), before)
  3946	                # AC-08 property 2: logout/delete.
  3947	                auth.unlink()
  3948	                after_logout = cat._codex_auth_signature()
  3949	                self.assertNotEqual(after_logout, before)
  3950	                self.assertEqual(after_logout, "")
  3951	
  3952	    def test_adr0043_ac07_ac08_claude_code_auth_signature_ignores_material_and_the_unrelated_mcp_block(self):
  3953	        """AC-07/AC-08 for claude-code, PLUS the trap the context pack names explicitly:
  3954	        `~/.claude/.credentials.json` also holds `mcpOAuth` (today a Vercel token) in the
  3955	        SAME file -- a refresh of that UNRELATED MCP credential must never move this
  3956	        signature, only a real Claude Code OAuth refresh (harmless, ignored) or a real
  3957	        logout (file deleted, signature changes) may."""
  3958	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  3959	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  3960	            home = Path(td); claude_dir = home / ".claude"; claude_dir.mkdir()
  3961	            creds = claude_dir / ".credentials.json"
  3962	            base_oauth = {"accessToken": "secret-before", "expiresAt": 1000, "rateLimitTier": "tier1",
  3963	                         "refreshToken": "refresh-before", "refreshTokenExpiresAt": 2000,
  3964	                         "scopes": ["user:inference"], "subscriptionType": "max"}
  3965	            creds.write_text(json.dumps({"claudeAiOauth": base_oauth,
  3966	                                         "mcpOAuth": {"vercel": {"accessToken": "vercel-secret-before"}}}))
  3967	            with mock.patch.object(cat.Path, "home", return_value=home):
  3968	                before = cat._claude_code_auth_signature()
  3969	                self.assertTrue(before)
  3970	                self.assertNotIn("secret-before", before)
  3971	                # AC-08 property 1: a Claude Code refresh rotates accessToken/expiresAt/
  3972	                # refreshToken/refreshTokenExpiresAt, keeps the three fields read here.
  3973	                refreshed_oauth = {**base_oauth, "accessToken": "secret-AFTER", "expiresAt": 9999,
  3974	                                   "refreshToken": "refresh-AFTER", "refreshTokenExpiresAt": 8888}
  3975	                creds.write_text(json.dumps({"claudeAiOauth": refreshed_oauth,
  3976	                                             "mcpOAuth": {"vercel": {"accessToken": "vercel-secret-before"}}}))
  3977	                self.assertEqual(cat._claude_code_auth_signature(), before)
  3978	                # The trap itself: an UNRELATED MCP token refresh must never move this.
  3979	                creds.write_text(json.dumps({"claudeAiOauth": refreshed_oauth,
  3980	                                             "mcpOAuth": {"vercel": {"accessToken": "vercel-secret-AFTER-mcp-refresh"}}}))
  3981	                self.assertEqual(cat._claude_code_auth_signature(), before)
  3982	                # AC-08 property 2: logout/delete.
  3983	                creds.unlink()
  3984	                after_logout = cat._claude_code_auth_signature()
  3985	                self.assertNotEqual(after_logout, before)
  3986	                self.assertEqual(after_logout, "")
  3987

  4000	    def test_adr0043_ac07_binary_signature_covers_opencode_codex_and_claude(self):
  4001	        """Generalizes the pre-existing opencode-only binary signature (ADR-0034 AC-08)
  4002	        to `codex`/`claude` too -- a binary swap (mtime change) must move the signature,
  4003	        and an unresolvable binary must fail closed to a fixed per-name sentinel."""
  4004	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4005	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4006	            path = Path(td) / "codex"; path.write_text("#!/bin/sh\n"); path.chmod(0o755)
  4007	            with mock.patch.object(cat.shutil, "which", side_effect=lambda name: str(path) if name == "codex" else None):
  4008	                present = cat._binary_signature("codex")
  4009	                self.assertNotEqual(present, "codex:absent")
  4010	                older = os.stat(path)
  4011	                os.utime(path, (older.st_atime, older.st_mtime + 5))
  4012	                bumped = cat._binary_signature("codex")
  4013	                self.assertNotEqual(present, bumped)
  4014	                self.assertEqual(cat._binary_signature("claude"), "claude:absent")

  4016	    def test_adr0043_ac07_cache_key_folds_every_runtime_signature(self):
  4017	        """`_cache_key` is a SINGLE hash covering the whole probe-cache document (every
  4018	        runtime's pairs at once) -- a change in ANY one of the six new signatures must
  4019	        move the overall key, exactly like the pre-existing opencode-only binary
  4020	        signature already did."""
  4021	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4022	        base = cat._cache_key(self.config)
  4023	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_codex_auth_signature", return_value="different-codex"):
  4024	            self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(self.config), base)
  4025	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_claude_code_auth_signature", return_value="different-claude"):
  4026	            self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(self.config), base)
  4027	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_pi_auth_signature", return_value="different-pi"):
  4028	            self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(self.config), base)
  4029	        # Confirms _binary_signature is actually invoked for all THREE binaries, not
  4030	        # merely that mocking it changes the result (a name-blind mock could pass even
  4031	        # if `_cache_key` only ever called it for "opencode", the pre-existing call).
  4032	        calls = []
  4033	        original = cat._binary_signature
  4034	        def spy(name):
  4035	            calls.append(name)
  4036	            return original(name)
  4037	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_binary_signature", side_effect=spy):
  4038	            cat._cache_key(self.config)
  4039	        self.assertEqual(sorted(calls), ["claude", "codex", "opencode"])
  4040	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_binary_signature", side_effect=lambda name: f"{name}:forced-different"):
  4041	            self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(self.config), base)
  4042	
  4043	    def test_adr0043_ac07_cache_key_never_spawns_a_subprocess_for_the_new_signatures(self):
  4044	        """Trap #2 (context pack): AC-07 adds six new stat/local-read signatures to
  4045	        `_cache_key` without a single new subprocess -- the ONE subprocess the whole
  4046	        cache-key computation ever pays is `_live_opencode_auth_signature`, called by the
  4047	        CALLER (`probe_inventory`) and passed in as `auth_signature`, never invoked from
  4048	        `_cache_key` itself (the no-argument call below never touches it)."""
  4049	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4050	        def fail(*a, **k):
  4051	            raise AssertionError("no subprocess may run while computing _cache_key")
  4052	        with mock.patch.object(cat.subprocess, "run", side_effect=fail):
  4053	            cat._cache_key(self.config)  # must not raise

  4055	    def test_adr0043_ac07_ac08_codex_refresh_keeps_the_cache_warm_and_logout_invalidates_it_via_probe_inventory(self):
  4056	        """End-to-end AC-08 property proof, through `probe_inventory` itself (never a
  4057	        real `codex logout` -- the credential file is deleted directly, same discipline
  4058	        the task instructions require): a refresh keeps the cache warm (codex is never
  4059	        reprobed); a logout misses the cache on the VERY NEXT call, still well inside
  4060	        PROBE_CACHE_TTL (300s) -- unlike before this package, where only opencode's
  4061	        credential change was ever visible before the TTL expired."""
  4062	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4063	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4064	            bins, log = self._probe_stubs(td)
  4065	            home = Path(td) / "home"; (home / ".codex").mkdir(parents=True)
  4066	            auth = home / ".codex" / "auth.json"
  4067	            auth.write_text(json.dumps({"tokens": {"account_id": "acct_1", "access_token": "before"}}))
  4068	            cache_root = Path(td) / "root"; cache_root.mkdir(mode=0o700)
  4069	            old_path = os.environ["PATH"]; os.environ["PATH"] = f"{bins}:{old_path}"
  4070	            try:
  4071	                with mock.patch.object(cat.Path, "home", return_value=home):
  4072	                    cold = cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=cache_root, now=1000.0)
  4073	                    self.assertIn(("codex", "openai-codex"), cold)
  4074	                    auth.write_text(json.dumps({"tokens": {"account_id": "acct_1", "access_token": "AFTER"}}))
  4075	                    log.write_text("")
  4076	                    warm = cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=cache_root, now=1001.0)
  4077	                    self.assertEqual(warm, cold)
  4078	                    self.assertNotIn("bin/codex", log.read_text())
  4079	                    auth.unlink()
  4080	                    log.write_text("")
  4081	                    cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=cache_root, now=1002.0)
  4082	                    self.assertIn("bin/codex", log.read_text())
  4083	            finally:
  4084	                os.environ["PATH"] = old_path

  4113	    def test_adr0043_ac09_cache_schema_version_bump_invalidates_old_cache_documents(self):
  4114	        """AC-09: bumping `_CACHE_SCHEMA_VERSION` must change `_cache_key`'s output, and a
  4115	        cache document written and keyed under the OLD version must never read back as
  4116	        valid once the constant has moved -- there was no test for this before this
  4117	        package (context pack: "hoy no existe ninguno")."""
  4118	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4119	        # ADR-0043 AC-09: the bump actually happened (2 -> 3) -- this literal pins that a
  4120	        # future change reverting it (or forgetting a NEW bump when the key semantics
  4121	        # move again) is caught here, not only by the relative comparison below.
  4122	        self.assertEqual(cat._CACHE_SCHEMA_VERSION, 3)
  4123	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_CACHE_SCHEMA_VERSION", cat._CACHE_SCHEMA_VERSION - 1):
  4124	            old_key = cat._cache_key(self.config)
  4125	        new_key = cat._cache_key(self.config)
  4126	        self.assertNotEqual(old_key, new_key)
  4127	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4128	            cache_root = Path(td) / "root"; cache_root.mkdir(mode=0o700)
  4129	            doc = {"key": old_key, "at": 1000.0, "pairs": {}}
  4130	            path = cache_root / "probe-cache.json"; path.write_text(json.dumps(doc)); path.chmod(0o600)
  4131	            self.assertIsNone(cat._read_probe_cache(cache_root, new_key, 1000.5, self.config))
