  4201	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4202	            legacy_state = Path(td) / "legacy-state"; legacy_state.mkdir(mode=0o700)
  4203	            legacy_cache = legacy_state / "probe-cache.json"
  4204	            legacy_cache.write_text(json.dumps({"key": "stale", "at": 1.0, "pairs": {}}))
  4205	            os.chmod(legacy_cache, 0o600)
  4206	            routing_root = Path(td) / "routing-root"
  4207	            captured = {}
  4208	            def fake_probe_inventory(config, cache_root=None, **kwargs):
  4209	                captured["cache_root"] = cache_root
  4210	                return {}
  4211	            with mock.patch.object(app, "STATE_DIR", legacy_state), \
  4212	                 mock.patch.object(app, "ROUTING_TEST_ROOT", str(routing_root)), \
  4213	                 mock.patch("routing_core.catalog.probe_inventory", side_effect=fake_probe_inventory):
  4214	                self.assertEqual(app.cmd_doctor_all(), 0)
  4215	            self.assertEqual(captured["cache_root"], routing_root)
  4216	            self.assertFalse(legacy_cache.exists())
  4217	
  4218	    def test_adr0043_ac10_models_config_probe_cache_root_is_the_single_store_root_not_state_dir(self):
  4219	        root = models_config._probe_cache_root()
  4220	        self.assertTrue(str(root).endswith("/routing-v2"))
  4221	        self.assertNotEqual(root, Path.home() / ".local/state/set-agentes")
  4222	
  4223	    def test_adr0043_ac10_no_call_site_still_passes_the_legacy_state_dir_shaped_root(self):
  4224	        """Source-grep tripwire, same style as test_adr0034_ac02_... above: a future edit
  4225	        reintroducing `cache_root=STATE_DIR` (or the equivalent bare `Path.home() /
  4226	        ".local/state/set-agentes"` literal in models_config.py) would silently reopen
  4227	        the two-caches defect this whole package exists to close."""
  4228	        app_source = (ROOT / "ai/scripts/set_agents_app.py").read_text()
  4229	        self.assertNotIn("cache_root=STATE_DIR", app_source)
  4230	        self.assertEqual(app_source.count("cache_root=_probe_cache_root()"), 4)
  4231	        mc_source = (ROOT / "ai/scripts/models_config.py").read_text()
  4232	        self.assertNotIn('Path.home() / ".local/state/set-agentes"', mc_source)
  4233	        self.assertEqual(mc_source.count("cache_root=_probe_cache_root()"), 2)
  4234	
  4235	    # --------------------------------------------------- ADR-0034 (019 PKG-1, AC-02..09)
  4236	
  4237	    def test_adr0034_ac02_composition_and_recheck_derive_discovery_through_the_same_path(self):
  4238	        # AC-02: `service.py`'s initial composition and its `recheck` lambda MUST derive
  4239	        # the synthesized-routes universe through the exact same pure function
  4240	        # (`catalog.resolve_discovered_providers`, via `build_effective_snapshot`) --
  4241	        # deriving it two different ways is the hazard this test pins (a candidate
  4242	        # authorized on one derivation failing AUTHORIZATION_INVALID on the other,
  4243	        # purely from drift, never a real auth change). Source-grep tripwire, same style
  4244	        # already used in this file for the sort-key shape (014's own precedent).
  4245	        source = (ROOT / "ai/scripts/routing_core/service.py").read_text()
  4246	        self.assertIn("discovery_configured = isinstance(config, dict)", source)
  4247	        # Both the initial `snapshot, inferred_ids = ...` line and the `recheck` lambda
  4248	        # call `build_effective_snapshot` -- neither ever calls `build_snapshot` directly
  4249	        # when `discovery_configured` is true.
  4250	        self.assertIn("snapshot, inferred_ids = build_effective_snapshot(catalog_path, roster, config, inventory)", source)
  4251	        self.assertIn("recheck = lambda: build_effective_snapshot(", source)
  4252	        # F-03 (P1 repair): the plain, un-derived `build_snapshot(catalog_path, roster,
  4253	        # config), frozenset()` construction (the pre-017/pre-019 sealed shape) appears
  4254	        # EXACTLY ONCE in this module -- inside the `else` branch of `discovery_configured`
  4255	        # -- never as a second, silently-reintroduced call reachable when discovery IS
  4256	        # configured (the exact drift AC-02 forbids).
  4257	        plain_snapshot_call = "build_snapshot(catalog_path, roster, config), frozenset()"
  4258	        self.assertEqual(source.count(plain_snapshot_call), 1)
  4259	        else_branch = source.split("        else:\n", 1)[1]
  4260	        self.assertIn(plain_snapshot_call, else_branch)

-- ADR 0043 --
     1	# ADR-0043 — Qué prueba realmente un probe; firma de credencial por runtime; una sola caché
     2	
     3	- Estado: Accepted (2026-08-13). Feature 022-disponibilidad-real, PKG-3 (`P3-liveness-real`).
     4	  Depende de ADR-0042 (PKG-1/PKG-2, ya aceptado). No supersede nada.
     5	
     6	## Contexto
     7	
     8	Medición en vivo sobre el estado del repo antes de este paquete (`grep -rn`/lectura directa,
     9	`docs/specs/022-disponibilidad-real/evidence/P3-implementer.md` tiene el detalle mordido):
    10	
    11	1. **`_cache_key`** (`routing_core/catalog.py`, antes de este paquete) solo llevaba firma de
    12	   credencial de **opencode** (`_live_opencode_auth_signature`, un subprocess de
    13	   `opencode auth list --pure`, más `_opencode_binary_signature`, path+mtime del binario
    14	   `opencode`). Dar de baja una credencial en **codex**, **claude-code** o **pi** era invisible
    15	   hasta que `PROBE_CACHE_TTL` (300s) expiraba — 3 de 4 runtimes.
    16	2. **Dos cachés divergentes en disco, confirmado en vivo**: `--route-doctor` inspeccionaba
    17	   `STATE_DIR/probe-cache.json` (`~/.local/state/set-agentes/probe-cache.json`) mientras que la
    18	   vía de decisión real (`RoutingService.__init__`, vía `RoutingStore.ensure_cache_root()`)
    19	   lee/escribe `~/.local/state/set-agentes/routing-v2/probe-cache.json`. Mismo defecto,
    20	   duplicado además en `set_agents_app.py` (cuatro sitios) y `models_config.py` (dos sitios).
    21	3. **El probe responde una pregunta distinta de la que parece responder.** Medido en vivo:
    22	   `opencode auth list --pure` reporta la credencial OpenAI presente, `opencode models
    23	   openai --pure` lista modelos sin error, `--route-doctor` reporta `authenticated=true,
    24	   models_listable=6` — y la inferencia real por ese mismo par devuelve `Error: Provided
    25	   authentication token is expired.` El probe prueba **presencia de credencial**, no
    26	   **liveness**. Resolver esto (que un CLI acepte listar con un token vencido) es P5
    27	   (AC-16/AC-18), fuera de alcance de este paquete — pero el nombre no debe mentir sobre lo
    28	   que mide.
    29	4. **Los mismos campos, dos runtimes, dos respuestas.** `codex login status` reporta
    30	   `Logged in using ChatGPT` y `codex exec` funciona (PONG real) con la MISMA cuenta que
    31	   opencode reporta vencida. Cada runtime tiene su propio store de credenciales — valida
    32	   empíricamente que la firma tiene que ser **por runtime**, no un booleano global: una firma
    33	   global daría la respuesta equivocada en 3 de 4 casos.
    34	5. **Las credenciales, medidas (campos solamente, nunca valores)**:
    35	   - `~/.codex/auth.json`: `auth_mode`, `OPENAI_API_KEY`, `tokens.{id_token, access_token,
    36	     refresh_token, account_id}`, `last_refresh`. `tokens.account_id` es identidad de cuenta
    37	     real (una fila estable de la base de ChatGPT). `last_refresh` y los tres campos de
    38	     `tokens` que no son `account_id` **rotan** en cada refresh (medido: nombre del campo, no
    39	     su valor — el propio nombre `last_refresh` ya es evidencia de que el archivo se
    40	     reescribe periódicamente).
    41	   - `~/.claude/.credentials.json`: `claudeAiOauth.{accessToken, expiresAt, rateLimitTier,
    42	     refreshToken, refreshTokenExpiresAt, scopes, subscriptionType}` **y**, en el MISMO
    43	     archivo, `mcpOAuth.{...}` — hoy una credencial de un MCP de Vercel, sin relación alguna
    44	     con el proveedor Claude Code. Una firma ingenua ("hasheo el archivo entero" o "uso su
    45	     mtime") rota en cada refresh de ESE MCP, no solo de la credencial que importa — la
    46	     trampa medida y nombrada explícitamente en el context pack de este paquete.
    47	   - `~/.pi/agent/auth.json`: ya cubierto, sin subprocess, por `pi_auth_provider_keys()`
    48	     (spike Q2, 012) — un `frozenset` de nombres de proveedor, nunca valores.
    49	
    50	## Decisión
    51	
    52	### 1. Un probe prueba presencia de credencial, no liveness — se declara con esas palabras
    53	
    54	`route_doctor`, los docstrings de `probe_inventory`/`_probe_pairs` y esta ADR usan
    55	"credential presence" o su equivalente en español, nunca "liveness" ni "disponibilidad real
    56	del modelo". Resolver la brecha entre "el CLI lista sin error" y "el proveedor responde de
    57	verdad" es AC-16/AC-18 (P5) — este paquete no la resuelve, la nombra correctamente.
    58	
    59	### 2. Firma de credencial por runtime, toda `stat`/lectura local, cero subprocesos nuevos
    60	
    61	`_cache_key` pasa de una sola firma (opencode) a seis fuentes nuevas, todas locales:
    62	
    63	- **`_binary_signature(name)`** (generaliza `_opencode_binary_signature`, ADR-0034 AC-08):
    64	  path+mtime de `opencode`, `codex` y `claude` en PATH — un swap de binario invalida la
    65	  caché sin bump manual, ahora para los tres CLIs, no solo opencode.
    66	- **`_codex_auth_signature()`**: `~/.codex/auth.json`, SOLO `tokens.account_id` (identidad
    67	  real) y si `OPENAI_API_KEY` está presente (booleano, nunca el valor). Nunca
    68	  `access_token`/`refresh_token`/`id_token`/`last_refresh`.
    69	- **`_claude_code_auth_signature()`**: `~/.claude/.credentials.json`, SOLO
    70	  `claudeAiOauth.{scopes, subscriptionType, rateLimitTier}` — nunca `accessToken`/
    71	  `refreshToken`/`expiresAt`/`refreshTokenExpiresAt`, y nunca el archivo completo ni su
    72	  mtime (la trampa de `mcpOAuth` de arriba).
    73	- **`_pi_auth_signature()`**: `pi_auth_provider_keys()` (sin cambios) + `PI_PINNED_VERSION`
    74	  — un cambio de versión pineada también invalida, porque un release distinto de pi puede
    75	  leer/escribir un `auth.json` con forma distinta.
    76	
    77	Cada valor se hashea (`sha256`) antes de plegarse en `_cache_key` — nunca se loguea, nunca
    78	viaja en un envelope, nunca es el valor crudo de una credencial. Disciplina idéntica a
    79	`pi_auth_provider_keys` (`lstat`, nunca symlink, archivo regular, uid propio; cualquier
    80	sorpresa es fail-closed a la firma vacía, que nunca iguala a una firma real — cache miss,
    81	nunca un match fabricado).
    82	
    83	`_cache_key` sigue siendo una función pura sin I/O más allá de `stat`/lectura local — cero
    84	subprocesos nuevos, medido y probado
    85	(`test_adr0043_ac07_cache_key_never_spawns_a_subprocess_for_the_new_signatures`,
    86	`tests/test_routing.py`). El único subprocess de toda la composición de la clave sigue siendo
    87	`_live_opencode_auth_signature`, provisto por el LLAMADOR (`probe_inventory`) como
    88	`auth_signature`, nunca invocado desde `_cache_key` mismo.
    89	
    90	La clave es **una sola** para todo el documento de caché (que cubre los pares de los cuatro
    91	runtimes a la vez) — un cambio de credencial en CUALQUIER runtime invalida el documento
    92	entero, forzando un re-probe completo en la siguiente decisión. Es la superficie mínima que
    93	cierra el defecto sin introducir invalidación parcial (que exigiría rediseñar el formato del
    94	documento, fuera de alcance).
    95	
    96	### 3. Límite estructural de claude-code — aceptado por Federico, declarado, no disimulado
    97	
    98	`~/.claude/.credentials.json` **no tiene ningún campo de identidad de cuenta** — a diferencia
    99	de codex (`tokens.account_id`). Los únicos campos no-rotantes medidos (`scopes`,
   100	`subscriptionType`, `rateLimitTier`) son atributos del *grant*/plan OAuth (qué puede hacer
   101	esta cuenta, qué plan paga), no un identificador de cuenta. Consecuencia declarada: la firma
   102	de claude-code detecta **presencia y ausencia** (logout, borrado de credencial, archivo
   103	ausente) pero **no** un cambio de una cuenta a otra del mismo plan sin pasar por un logout
   104	intermedio. Cerrar esa brecha exigiría hashear `refreshToken` — **esta feature no lo hace**
   105	(AC-09 es explícito: "hashear el refreshToken no se hace"). El límite queda escrito acá y no
   106	se maquilla como si la firma detectara más de lo que detecta.
   107	
   108	### 4. Supuesto validado — con las salvedades de seguridad que impusieron el método
   109	
   110	El diseño de arriba depende de que `scopes`/`subscriptionType`/`rateLimitTier`
   111	(claude-code) y `tokens.account_id` (codex) NO roten en un refresh normal. Medición en vivo
   112	de la FORMA de ambos archivos, hecha en esta sesión (nombres de campo solamente, nunca
   113	valores — ver evidencia): confirma exactamente los campos que el context pack anticipaba,
   114	incluida la presencia real de `mcpOAuth` (hoy un token de Vercel) en el mismo archivo que
   115	`claudeAiOauth`.
   116	
   117	**No se forzó un refresh real contra el backend OAuth** (ni siquiera con `HOME` desviado a
   118	una copia aislada) para capturar un A/B de valores antes/después: muchos proveedores OAuth
   119	rotan el `refresh_token` del lado del servidor en cada uso (single-use rotating refresh
   120	tokens); forzar un refresh con el `refresh_token` REAL — aunque el resultado se escriba solo
   121	en una copia — puede invalidar del lado del servidor el `refresh_token` que sigue viviendo en
   122	el archivo real, dejando al usuario sin sesión válida. Es el mismo riesgo, con la misma
   123	severidad, que la instrucción explícita de no correr un `logout` real — tratado con la misma
   124	disciplina aunque no esté nombrado literalmente. **Queda marcado "sin verificar mediante
   125	refresh forzado en vivo"**, explícitamente, en vez de simulado como certeza.
   126	
   127	El diseño es robusto a esa incertidumbre por construcción: la firma de claude-code nunca lee
   128	`refreshToken`/`accessToken` (así que si esos SÍ rotan, es irrelevante), y los tres campos que
   129	sí lee son, por semántica de OAuth, atributos del grant, no del token — un argumento de diseño
   130	explícito, marcado como tal y no como medición. Si una medición futura contradice este
   131	argumento, el diseño de la firma de claude-code se revisa (mismo texto de advertencia que el
   132	context pack de PKG-3 ya dejó escrito).
   133	
   134	### 5. Una sola caché — la raíz del store, la legada se poda con la misma disciplina de seguridad
   135	
   136	`RoutingStore.root` (`store.py`, `~/.local/state/set-agentes/routing-v2`) es la única raíz de
   137	caché de probes desde este paquete. Los seis sitios que antes pasaban
   138	`cache_root=STATE_DIR` (o el equivalente `Path.home() / ".local/state/set-agentes"` en
   139	`models_config.py`) migran a `_probe_cache_root()` — un helper por módulo que expone
   140	`RoutingStore().root` (atributo puro, sin I/O al construirse, misma pereza que `STATE_DIR`
   141	siempre tuvo) para no crear directorios como efecto secundario de una lectura diagnóstica.
   142	`route_doctor`, `--doctor-all` y el panel "Estado general" (las tres superficies "vidriera"
   143	que un humano corre para ver qué detecta el harness) además podan, con el mismo esfuerzo
   144	best-effort, el archivo legado sobreviviente (`prune_legacy_probe_cache`, misma disciplina de
   145	`_validate_cache_dir` que `_write_probe_cache` ya usaba: directorio 0700 de este uid, sin
   146	symlink; archivo regular de este uid, sin symlink — cualquier sorpresa deja el archivo
   147	intacto, nunca se borra a ciegas). Nunca toca el directorio `STATE_DIR` en sí (que también
   148	aloja `config.toml`/`model-preference.toml`/etc.), solo el archivo `probe-cache.json` legado.
   149	
   150	### 6. `_CACHE_SCHEMA_VERSION` 2 → 3, con test — no existía ninguno antes
   151	
   152	Bump porque la SEMÁNTICA de la clave cambió (seis fuentes nuevas), aunque la forma del
   153	documento persistido (`{"key", "at", "pairs"}`) no se movió — el propio docstring de
   154	`_CACHE_SCHEMA_VERSION` ya distinguía "content shape" de "key semantics" y este bump cae en
   155	la segunda categoría. Antes de este paquete no existía ningún test que probara que un bump
   156	invalida una caché escrita bajo la versión anterior; ahora sí
   157	(`test_adr0043_ac09_cache_schema_version_bump_invalidates_old_cache_documents`).
   158	
   159	### 7. `pi` descubre modelos, no credenciales — reafirmado, no reabierto
   160	
   161	Sin cambios de este paquete: `pi_auth_provider_keys()` solo confirma claves YA conocidas por
   162	otros runtimes (sus dos proveedores, `openai-codex`/`anthropic`, ya están auditados por
   163	codex/claude-code) — pi nunca puede aportar un proveedor nuevo al inventario. `_pi_auth_
   164	signature()` (arriba) reutiliza esa misma función sin ampliar lo que lee.
   165	
   166	## Alternativas rechazadas
   167	
   168	- **Hashear `refreshToken` de claude-code para recuperar identidad de cuenta**: rechazado —
   169	  AC-09 lo prohíbe explícitamente (`refreshToken` es material de credencial, no un
   170	  identificador estable; rota, y hashear un secreto rotante que además es la llave para
   171	  obtener un `accessToken` nuevo amplía la superficie sensible sin necesidad).
   172	- **Hashear el archivo `~/.claude/.credentials.json` completo, o usar su mtime**: rechazado
   173	  por la trampa medida de `mcpOAuth` — rotaría en cada refresh de un MCP sin relación,
   174	  anulando la caché en cada decisión por una razón ajena a la credencial del proveedor.
   175	- **Forzar un refresh real (incluso sandboxed vía `HOME`) para la captura A/B**: rechazado por
   176	  el riesgo de rotación de `refresh_token` del lado del servidor invalidando la sesión real
   177	  del usuario — mismo nivel de severidad que un `logout` real, tratado igual aunque la
   178	  consigna no lo nombrara literalmente.
   179	- **Leer el propio `~/.local/share/opencode/auth.json` de opencode para eliminar su
   180	  subprocess** (mejora nombrada como opcional en el context pack): rechazada para este
   181	  paquete — reimplementar la normalización que `opencode auth list --pure` ya hace (mapeo de
   182	  nombres de proveedor, distinción `●`/`○` pendiente-vs-confirmado) sobre un formato de
   183	  archivo no documentado reabre el mismo riesgo de parseo no verificado que ADR-0034 M-1 ya
   184	  evita para los CLI ids — y el subprocess existente ya es el único de toda la composición de
   185	  la clave, no el problema que este paquete existe para resolver. Queda declarada, no tomada.
   186	- **Un solo booleano de credencial por runtime, sin distinguir identidad**: rechazado por la
   187	  medición del punto 4 del Contexto — una firma que solo mira "¿hay algo logueado?" no
   188	  distinguiría un cambio de cuenta de codex (que sí tiene `account_id`) de una simple
   189	  presencia, perdiendo la única señal de identidad real que un runtime SÍ ofrece.
   190	- **Invalidación parcial por runtime (una sub-clave por par en vez de una clave global)**:
   191	  rechazado por alcance — exigiría rediseñar el formato del documento persistido
   192	  (`{"key", "at", "pairs"}`), y un re-probe completo tras CUALQUIER cambio de credencial ya
   193	  cierra el defecto medido (300s → inmediato) sin ese costo de diseño.
   194	
   195	## Consecuencias
   196	
   197	- Dar de baja (o dar de alta) una credencial en cualquiera de los cuatro runtimes se nota en
   198	  la decisión siguiente, no hasta 300s después — el criterio de cierre de la spec 022 para
   199	  este paquete.
   200	- `--route-doctor` reporta sobre la MISMA caché que la vía de decisión usa; el archivo legado
   201	  se poda solo, sin intervención manual, con la disciplina de seguridad ya establecida.
   202	- El límite de identidad de claude-code queda escrito y es parte del contrato, no una
   203	  sorpresa futura.
   204	- La palabra "liveness" en el nombre de este paquete (`P3-liveness-real`) queda corregida por
   205	  esta ADR: lo que se cierra es presencia de credencial por runtime, no verificación de que el
   206	  proveedor responda — eso sigue siendo P5.
   207	
   208	## Evidencia
   209	
   210	`docs/specs/022-disponibilidad-real/evidence/P3-implementer.md`.

-- ADR index ref --
50:| [0043](0043-que-prueba-un-probe.md) | Un probe prueba presencia de credencial, no liveness (se declara así); firma de credencial por runtime en `_cache_key` (codex/claude-code/pi/opencode), cero subprocesos nuevos; límite estructural de claude-code (sin identidad de cuenta) declarado; una sola caché de probes en la raíz del store, la legada se poda con la disciplina de `_write_probe_cache` | Accepted | 2026-08-13 | — | — |

codex
Ya confirmé que el cambio toca la clave y la caché compartida. Ahora voy a ejecutar los chequeos dirigidos y los escenarios con credenciales sintéticas; antes, voy a aislar también los binarios de probe para que ningún comando consulte el `HOME` real.

                  [--feature-id ID] [--out FILE] [--vault-doctor] [--repair]
                  [--include-notes] [--private] [--company NAME]
                  [--model-preference-set CLASS] [--provider NAME]
                  [--model-preference-role-override ROLE CLASS]
                  [--model-preference-show]
                  [--model-pin-set ROLE PROVIDER/MODEL]
                  [--model-pin-clear ROLE]

set-agents: unified console app for the SET-AGENTS harness (gentle-ai style).
A TTY menu plus a scriptable CLI over the same primitives: install/repair
(install.sh), self-update (git pull --ff-only + managed reinstall), model
routing (setup-models.sh), and — in later sections — the optional tools
catalog, MCP servers, and Claude Code plugins.

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --status              estado en una línea (APP_STATUS ...)
  --route-explain TASK_CLASS
  --routing-report
  --route-doctor        ADR-0035, solo lectura: por par OpenCode, auth/modelos
                        listados/billing y diagnóstico del cache; expone
                        credenciales sin CLI id verificado (M-1)
  --route-decide FILE   descriptor JSON ('-' = stdin); decide y, para writers,
                        autoriza
  --route-dispatched RUN_ID
  --route-terminal RUN_ID OUTCOME
  --route-quota-exhausted RUN_ID
  --quota-failover-e2e  AC-06 live gate; bloquea hasta verificar una
                        suscripción agotada controlada
  --quota-error JSON
  --routing-open-runs
  --routing-recent-writers
  --routing-decisions   tail del log de decisiones por spawn (ADR-0031, solo
                        lectura)
  --limit LIMIT         con --routing-decisions: máximo de entradas (default
                        50)
  --routing-migrate     migra explícitamente la DB de routing al schema actual
  --project DIR         ancla explícita del proyecto para ruteo
  --scaffold [DIR]      crea el estado portable mínimo del proyecto
  --fresh-probes        con --route-decide: saltea el cache de probes
  --latency-ms LATENCY_MS
                        con --route-terminal: latencia observada
  --usage JSON          con --route-terminal: uso/costo del spawn
  --json                salida JSON para comandos de observabilidad
  --check-update
  --update
  --yes
  --no-install
  --auto-update {on,off}
  --tools               TOOL <name> installed=yes/no
  --tools-install NAME
  --dry-run
  --mcp                 MCP <name> harness=<h> state=...
  --mcp-add NAME
  --mcp-remove NAME
  --mcp-on NAME
  --mcp-off NAME
  --harness {opencode,claude,codex,cursor,gemini,pi}
  --doctor              chequeo redactado del harness (usar con --harness pi)
  --doctor-all          qué detecta esta máquina: harnesses, CLIs y
                        proveedores autenticados
  --plugins
  --plugin-on NAME
  --plugin-off NAME
  --vault-init DIR      crea el vault Obsidian de la empresa en DIR/obsidian
  --vault-link PROYECTO
                        linkea docs/notas del proyecto al vault
  --vault DIR           vault explícito para --vault-link
  --context             hub + contexto de empresa + nota del proyecto + qué
                        falta (solo lectura)
  --graph               grafo de ejecución (mermaid): findings, reviews,
                        verificaciones, repairs, blockers (solo lectura)
  --feature-id ID       con --graph: limita a esta feature (repetible; sin
                        ninguna, todas)
  --out FILE            con --graph: escribe el mermaid en FILE en vez de
                        stdout
  --vault-doctor        estado del vault: symlinks sanos, drift, no-
                        registrados (report-only)
  --repair              con --vault-doctor --project: aplica lo que el --dry-
                        run inmediatamente anterior confirmó
  --include-notes       con --vault-doctor --project --dry-run: docs/notas
                        queda DENTRO del git del proyecto tras migrar (opt-out
                        explícito; por defecto DEC-5 lo excluye)
  --private             con --vault-link: las notas viven en el vault y el
                        repo queda con un symlink excluido de git
  --company NAME
  --model-preference-set CLASS
                        escribe [preference].CLASS en model-preference.toml;
                        combinar con --provider (repetible, ordenado)
  --provider NAME       con --model-preference-set: proveedor, repetible, el
                        orden en la línea de comando es la preferencia
  --model-preference-role-override ROLE CLASS
                        escribe [role_override].ROLE = CLASS en model-
                        preference.toml
  --model-preference-show
                        lee model-preference.toml (solo lectura)
  --model-pin-set ROLE PROVIDER/MODEL
                        escribe [model_pin].ROLE en model-preference.toml
                        (ROLE puede ser '*')
  --model-pin-clear ROLE
                        borra [model_pin].ROLE de model-preference.toml

Primera vez: leé README.md — explica qué vas a ver según tu sistema operativo.
Dos verbos más (ADR-0038, interceptados antes de este parser, no listados
arriba): --tools-propose <name> --kind cli|mcp|skill --detect <bin>
--install-<method> "<cmd>" --why "<motivo>" (valida y imprime la pregunta
consolidada, nunca instala) y --tools-approve <name> (la aprobación humana --
nunca la corre un agente, sea cual sea su rol).

  3921	    def test_adr0043_ac07_ac08_codex_auth_signature_ignores_rotating_material_reacts_to_logout(self):
  3922	        """AC-07 (stat-only, no subprocess) + AC-08's two properties for codex: a refresh
  3923	        (rotates last_refresh/every token field, keeps tokens.account_id and the
  3924	        OPENAI_API_KEY presence bit) never moves the signature; a logout (file deleted)
  3925	        moves it immediately, never a fabricated match with the empty sentinel."""
  3926	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  3927	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  3928	            home = Path(td); codex_dir = home / ".codex"; codex_dir.mkdir()
  3929	            auth = codex_dir / "auth.json"
  3930	            auth.write_text(json.dumps({
  3931	                "OPENAI_API_KEY": None, "auth_mode": "chatgpt", "last_refresh": "2026-08-12T00:00:00Z",
  3932	                "tokens": {"access_token": "secret-before", "account_id": "acct_123",
  3933	                           "id_token": "id-before", "refresh_token": "refresh-before"},
  3934	            }))
  3935	            with mock.patch.object(cat.Path, "home", return_value=home):
  3936	                before = cat._codex_auth_signature()
  3937	                self.assertTrue(before)
  3938	                self.assertNotIn("acct_123", before); self.assertNotIn("secret-before", before)
  3939	                # AC-08 property 1: refresh.
  3940	                auth.write_text(json.dumps({
  3941	                    "OPENAI_API_KEY": None, "auth_mode": "chatgpt", "last_refresh": "2026-08-12T00:05:00Z",
  3942	                    "tokens": {"access_token": "secret-AFTER", "account_id": "acct_123",
  3943	                               "id_token": "id-AFTER", "refresh_token": "refresh-AFTER"},
  3944	                }))
  3945	                self.assertEqual(cat._codex_auth_signature(), before)
  3946	                # AC-08 property 2: logout/delete.
  3947	                auth.unlink()
  3948	                after_logout = cat._codex_auth_signature()
  3949	                self.assertNotEqual(after_logout, before)
  3950	                self.assertEqual(after_logout, "")
  3951	
  3952	    def test_adr0043_ac07_ac08_claude_code_auth_signature_ignores_material_and_the_unrelated_mcp_block(self):
  3953	        """AC-07/AC-08 for claude-code, PLUS the trap the context pack names explicitly:
  3954	        `~/.claude/.credentials.json` also holds `mcpOAuth` (today a Vercel token) in the
  3955	        SAME file -- a refresh of that UNRELATED MCP credential must never move this
  3956	        signature, only a real Claude Code OAuth refresh (harmless, ignored) or a real
  3957	        logout (file deleted, signature changes) may."""
  3958	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  3959	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  3960	            home = Path(td); claude_dir = home / ".claude"; claude_dir.mkdir()
  3961	            creds = claude_dir / ".credentials.json"
  3962	            base_oauth = {"accessToken": "secret-before", "expiresAt": 1000, "rateLimitTier": "tier1",
  3963	                         "refreshToken": "refresh-before", "refreshTokenExpiresAt": 2000,
  3964	                         "scopes": ["user:inference"], "subscriptionType": "max"}
  3965	            creds.write_text(json.dumps({"claudeAiOauth": base_oauth,
  3966	                                         "mcpOAuth": {"vercel": {"accessToken": "vercel-secret-before"}}}))
  3967	            with mock.patch.object(cat.Path, "home", return_value=home):
  3968	                before = cat._claude_code_auth_signature()
  3969	                self.assertTrue(before)
  3970	                self.assertNotIn("secret-before", before)
  3971	                # AC-08 property 1: a Claude Code refresh rotates accessToken/expiresAt/
  3972	                # refreshToken/refreshTokenExpiresAt, keeps the three fields read here.
  3973	                refreshed_oauth = {**base_oauth, "accessToken": "secret-AFTER", "expiresAt": 9999,
  3974	                                   "refreshToken": "refresh-AFTER", "refreshTokenExpiresAt": 8888}
  3975	                creds.write_text(json.dumps({"claudeAiOauth": refreshed_oauth,
  3976	                                             "mcpOAuth": {"vercel": {"accessToken": "vercel-secret-before"}}}))
  3977	                self.assertEqual(cat._claude_code_auth_signature(), before)
  3978	                # The trap itself: an UNRELATED MCP token refresh must never move this.
  3979	                creds.write_text(json.dumps({"claudeAiOauth": refreshed_oauth,
  3980	                                             "mcpOAuth": {"vercel": {"accessToken": "vercel-secret-AFTER-mcp-refresh"}}}))
  3981	                self.assertEqual(cat._claude_code_auth_signature(), before)
  3982	                # AC-08 property 2: logout/delete.
  3983	                creds.unlink()
  3984	                after_logout = cat._claude_code_auth_signature()
  3985	                self.assertNotEqual(after_logout, before)
  3986	                self.assertEqual(after_logout, "")

  4000	    def test_adr0043_ac07_binary_signature_covers_opencode_codex_and_claude(self):
  4001	        """Generalizes the pre-existing opencode-only binary signature (ADR-0034 AC-08)
  4002	        to `codex`/`claude` too -- a binary swap (mtime change) must move the signature,
  4003	        and an unresolvable binary must fail closed to a fixed per-name sentinel."""
  4004	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4005	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4006	            path = Path(td) / "codex"; path.write_text("#!/bin/sh\n"); path.chmod(0o755)
  4007	            with mock.patch.object(cat.shutil, "which", side_effect=lambda name: str(path) if name == "codex" else None):
  4008	                present = cat._binary_signature("codex")
  4009	                self.assertNotEqual(present, "codex:absent")
  4010	                older = os.stat(path)
  4011	                os.utime(path, (older.st_atime, older.st_mtime + 5))
  4012	                bumped = cat._binary_signature("codex")
  4013	                self.assertNotEqual(present, bumped)
  4014	                self.assertEqual(cat._binary_signature("claude"), "claude:absent")
  4015	
  4016	    def test_adr0043_ac07_cache_key_folds_every_runtime_signature(self):
  4017	        """`_cache_key` is a SINGLE hash covering the whole probe-cache document (every
  4018	        runtime's pairs at once) -- a change in ANY one of the six new signatures must
  4019	        move the overall key, exactly like the pre-existing opencode-only binary
  4020	        signature already did."""
  4021	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4022	        base = cat._cache_key(self.config)
  4023	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_codex_auth_signature", return_value="different-codex"):
  4024	            self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(self.config), base)
  4025	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_claude_code_auth_signature", return_value="different-claude"):
  4026	            self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(self.config), base)
  4027	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_pi_auth_signature", return_value="different-pi"):
  4028	            self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(self.config), base)
  4029	        # Confirms _binary_signature is actually invoked for all THREE binaries, not
  4030	        # merely that mocking it changes the result (a name-blind mock could pass even
  4031	        # if `_cache_key` only ever called it for "opencode", the pre-existing call).
  4032	        calls = []
  4033	        original = cat._binary_signature
  4034	        def spy(name):
  4035	            calls.append(name)
  4036	            return original(name)
  4037	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_binary_signature", side_effect=spy):
  4038	            cat._cache_key(self.config)
  4039	        self.assertEqual(sorted(calls), ["claude", "codex", "opencode"])
  4040	        with mock.patch.object(cat, "_binary_signature", side_effect=lambda name: f"{name}:forced-different"):
  4041	            self.assertNotEqual(cat._cache_key(self.config), base)
  4042	
  4043	    def test_adr0043_ac07_cache_key_never_spawns_a_subprocess_for_the_new_signatures(self):
  4044	        """Trap #2 (context pack): AC-07 adds six new stat/local-read signatures to
  4045	        `_cache_key` without a single new subprocess -- the ONE subprocess the whole
  4046	        cache-key computation ever pays is `_live_opencode_auth_signature`, called by the
  4047	        CALLER (`probe_inventory`) and passed in as `auth_signature`, never invoked from
  4048	        `_cache_key` itself (the no-argument call below never touches it)."""
  4049	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4050	        def fail(*a, **k):
  4051	            raise AssertionError("no subprocess may run while computing _cache_key")
  4052	        with mock.patch.object(cat.subprocess, "run", side_effect=fail):
  4053	            cat._cache_key(self.config)  # must not raise
  4054	
  4055	    def test_adr0043_ac07_ac08_codex_refresh_keeps_the_cache_warm_and_logout_invalidates_it_via_probe_inventory(self):
  4056	        """End-to-end AC-08 property proof, through `probe_inventory` itself (never a
  4057	        real `codex logout` -- the credential file is deleted directly, same discipline
  4058	        the task instructions require): a refresh keeps the cache warm (codex is never
  4059	        reprobed); a logout misses the cache on the VERY NEXT call, still well inside
  4060	        PROBE_CACHE_TTL (300s) -- unlike before this package, where only opencode's
  4061	        credential change was ever visible before the TTL expired."""
  4062	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4063	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4064	            bins, log = self._probe_stubs(td)
  4065	            home = Path(td) / "home"; (home / ".codex").mkdir(parents=True)
  4066	            auth = home / ".codex" / "auth.json"
  4067	            auth.write_text(json.dumps({"tokens": {"account_id": "acct_1", "access_token": "before"}}))
  4068	            cache_root = Path(td) / "root"; cache_root.mkdir(mode=0o700)
  4069	            old_path = os.environ["PATH"]; os.environ["PATH"] = f"{bins}:{old_path}"
  4070	            try:
  4071	                with mock.patch.object(cat.Path, "home", return_value=home):
  4072	                    cold = cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=cache_root, now=1000.0)
  4073	                    self.assertIn(("codex", "openai-codex"), cold)
  4074	                    auth.write_text(json.dumps({"tokens": {"account_id": "acct_1", "access_token": "AFTER"}}))
  4075	                    log.write_text("")
  4076	                    warm = cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=cache_root, now=1001.0)

  4080	                    log.write_text("")

  4133	    def test_adr0043_ac10_prune_legacy_probe_cache_removes_only_the_validated_sibling_file(self):
  4134	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4135	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4136	            root = Path(td) / "legacy"; root.mkdir(mode=0o700)

  4147	    def test_adr0043_ac10_prune_legacy_probe_cache_never_crosses_a_symlink_or_a_wrong_mode_directory(self):
  4148	        """Same `_validate_cache_dir`/file-validation discipline as `_write_probe_cache`
  4149	        (`test_probe_cache_ignores_unvalidated_directory` above) -- mirrored exactly for
  4150	        the pruning path, in both directions (a symlinked/wrong-mode directory, and a
  4151	        symlinked file inside an otherwise-valid one)."""
  4152	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  4153	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  4154	            real = Path(td) / "real"; real.mkdir(mode=0o700)
  4155	            (real / "probe-cache.json").write_text("{}"); os.chmod(real / "probe-cache.json", 0o600)
  4156	            link = Path(td) / "link"; link.symlink_to(real)
  4157	            self.assertFalse(cat.prune_legacy_probe_cache(link))
  4158	            self.assertTrue((real / "probe-cache.json").exists())
  4159	            wrong_mode = Path(td) / "wrong"; wrong_mode.mkdir(mode=0o755)
  4160	            (wrong_mode / "probe-cache.json").write_text("{}"); os.chmod(wrong_mode / "probe-cache.json", 0o600)
  4161	            self.assertFalse(cat.prune_legacy_probe_cache(wrong_mode))
  4162	            self.assertTrue((wrong_mode / "probe-cache.json").exists())
  4163	            valid = Path(td) / "valid"; valid.mkdir(mode=0o700)
  4164	            target = Path(td) / "target-secret.json"; target.write_text("{}")
  4165	            (valid / "probe-cache.json").symlink_to(target)
  4166	            self.assertFalse(cat.prune_legacy_probe_cache(valid))
  4167	            self.assertTrue(target.exists())

   602	def cmd_route_decide(source, human=False, fresh=False):
   603	    allowed = {"role", "task_class", "risk", "review_of_run_id", "selected_runtime", "feature_id", "package_id"}
   604	    try:
   605	        raw = sys.stdin.read() if source == "-" else Path(source).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   606	        doc = json.loads(raw)
   607	        if not isinstance(doc, dict) or set(doc) - allowed: raise ValueError
   608	        role, task_class = doc.get("role"), doc.get("task_class")
   609	        if not isinstance(role, str) or task_class not in routing.TASK_CLASSES: raise ValueError
   610	        req_risk = doc.get("risk", "low"); runtime = doc.get("selected_runtime", "opencode")
   611	        review_of = doc.get("review_of_run_id")
   612	        feature_id = doc.get("feature_id"); package_id = doc.get("package_id")
   613	        for value in (req_risk, runtime) + tuple(v for v in (review_of, feature_id, package_id) if v is not None):
   614	            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value: raise ValueError
   615	        # F01: a descriptor risk/runtime outside the closed enum is a PARSE failure (exit 2
   616	        # ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID) — it never reaches the service to degrade into FACTS_INCOMPLETE.
   617	        if req_risk not in routing.RISK_ORDER or runtime not in routing.SELECTED_RUNTIMES: raise ValueError
   618	    except (OSError, ValueError):
   619	        _routing_output(routing.cli_envelope(False, "route-decide", {}, (), ("ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID",)), human); return 2
   620	    try:
   621	        config = models_config.load_config(ROOT / "models.toml")
   622	        roster = models_config.load_roster(ROOT / "roles.tsv")
   623	        row = next((item for item in roster if item["role"] == role), None)
   624	        if row is None:
   625	            _routing_output(routing.cli_envelope(False, "route-decide", {}, (), ("FACTS_INCOMPLETE",)), human); return 1
   626	        role_class = _role_class_of(row)
   627	        # AM-1 (ADR-0006): capability decides write access and tools; task_class decides criticality
   628	        # and the base risk; the descriptor risk can only RAISE (combined in the service); context
   629	        # flags derive from the active package's context pack.
   630	        writer = role_class == "writer"
   631	        criticality = task_class if task_class in routing.CRITICAL else ""
   632	        base_risk = "high" if (criticality or task_class == "incident") else "low"
   633	        needs_context = bool(criticality) or base_risk == "high" or req_risk == "high"
   634	        context_ok, resolved_feature, resolved_package = _resolve_context_pack(feature_id, package_id)
   635	        if needs_context and context_ok is None:
   636	            # F03d: the harness itself could not narrow the default resolution to exactly one
   637	            # actively-executing package — distinct from NO_ELIGIBLE_ROUTE (a real catalog
   638	            # exclusion), and distinct from a resolved-but-missing pack (plain CONTEXT_MISSING).
   639	            data = {"feature_id": resolved_feature, "package_id": resolved_package, "context_ok": None}
   640	            _routing_output(routing.cli_envelope(False, "route-decide", data, (), ("CONTEXT_UNRESOLVED",)), human)
   641	            return 1
   642	        context_flag = bool(context_ok)
   643	        unverified_review = role_class == "review" and not review_of
   644	        simulate = not writer and not (role_class == "review" and review_of)
   645	        config = _config_with_model_preference(config, roster)
   646	        service = routing.compose(config, roster, simulate=simulate, fresh_probes=fresh,
   647	                                  store=None if simulate else _routing_store())
   648	        request = routing.TaskRequest(role=role, operation="inspection" if task_class == "inspection" else "change",
   649	                                      task_class=task_class, risk=req_risk, selected_runtime=runtime)
   650	        facts = service._observe_for_invocation(role=role, operation=request.operation, task_class=task_class,
   651	            read_write="write" if writer else "read", write_started=False,
   652	            risk=base_risk, criticality=criticality, affected_surfaces=(),
   653	            required_tools=("read", "shell", "write") if writer else ("read",),
   654	            context_required=needs_context,
   655	            context_present=context_flag, critical_coverage=context_flag, selected_runtime=runtime)
   656	        decision = service.route(request, facts, review_of, unverified_review=unverified_review)
   657	        tier = next((r.tier for r in service.snapshot.routes if r.route_id == decision.route_id), None)
   658	        data = decision.to_dict(); data["tier"] = tier; data["role_class"] = role_class
   659	        # F03: the effective (feature_id, package_id, context_ok) is always in the envelope,
   660	        # even when context wasn't needed, for audit.
   661	        data["feature_id"] = resolved_feature; data["package_id"] = resolved_package; data["context_ok"] = context_flag
   662	        # ADR-0031: mint a per-decision id (parallel to run1_/proj1_) and append the whole
   663	        # decision to the sidecar log — simulate included, which is exactly the class of
   664	        # decision that previously left no durable trace (store=None above stays as-is).
   665	        decision_id = "dec1_" + secrets.token_hex(16)
   666	        data["decision_id"] = decision_id
   667	        # ADR-0032: which of the selection paths produced this identity — "pin" (a user
   668	        # [model_pin] override was selected) or "dynamic" (the router's own pick; a
   669	        # configured-but-ineligible pin shows up as MODEL_PIN_UNAVAILABLE in
   670	        # reason_codes and still counts as dynamic). The THIRD path, the curated static
   671	        # fallback, only exists at materialization time and is recorded on the spawn
   672	        # record as MODEL_STATIC_FALLBACK (ADR-0030), never here.
   673	        selection_path = ("pin" if any(code.startswith("MODEL_PINNED") for code in decision.reason_codes)
   674	                          else "dynamic")
   675	        data["selection_path"] = selection_path
   676	        ok, exit_code = _decide_status(decision)
   677	        _append_decision_log({
   678	            "at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   679	            "decision_id": decision_id, "run_id": data.get("run_id"),
   680	            "project_key": PROJECT_KEY, "role": role, "task_class": task_class,
   681	            "risk": req_risk, "role_class": role_class, "simulate": simulate,
   682	            "route_id": data.get("route_id"), "runtime": data.get("runtime"),
   683	            "provider": data.get("provider"), "model": data.get("model"),
   684	            "family": data.get("family"), "effort": data.get("effort"), "tier": tier,
   685	            "feature_id": resolved_feature, "package_id": resolved_package,
   686	            "reason_codes": list(decision.reason_codes),
   687	            "execution_enabled": bool(data.get("execution_enabled")),
   688	            "selection_path": selection_path,
   689	        })
   690	        _routing_output(routing.cli_envelope(ok, "route-decide", data, (), decision.reason_codes), human)
   691	        return exit_code
   692	    except models_config.ModelsError:
   693	        _routing_output(routing.cli_envelope(False, "route-decide", {}, (), ("ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID",)), human); return 2
   694	    except ModelPreferenceError as exc:
   695	        _routing_output(routing.cli_envelope(False, "route-decide", {"message": str(exc)}, (), ("ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID",)), human); return 2
   696	    # SEC-A02: any unvalidated internal edge (a malformed feature-state.json field, an
   697	    # out-of-range value reaching the store) degrades to ROUTING_UNAVAILABLE — never an
   698	    # uncaught traceback breaking the schema-2 envelope / one-JSON-line contract.
   699	    except (routing.RoutingError, OSError, TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
   700	        _routing_output(routing.cli_envelope(False, "route-decide", {}, (), ("ROUTING_UNAVAILABLE",)), human); return 1
   701	
   702	
   703	def _lifecycle_command(name, run_id, action, human):
   704	    if not isinstance(run_id, str) or not _RUN_ID.fullmatch(run_id):
   705	        _routing_output(routing.cli_envelope(False, name, {}, (), ("ROUTING_INPUT_INVALID",)), human); return 2
   706	    try:
   707	        result = action(_routing_store())
   708	        _routing_output(routing.cli_envelope(True, name, result, (), ()), human); return 0
   709	    except routing.RoutingError as exc:
   710	        _routing_output(routing.cli_envelope(False, name, {}, (), (str(exc),)), human); return 1
  2610	            finally:
  2611	                set_agents_app.ROOT, set_agents_app.PROJECT_ROOT = old_root, old_project
  2612	
  2613	    def test_schema_four_warning_is_informational_and_never_auto_migrates(self):
  2614	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  2615	            root = Path(td)
  2616	            project = root / "git-only"
  2617	            project.mkdir(); (project / ".git").mkdir()
  2618	            routing_root = root / "routing"
  2619	            store = routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(routing_root)
  2620	            store._safe_dir(create=True)
  2621	            fd = os.open(store.db_path, os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_RDWR, 0o600)
  2622	            os.close(fd)
  2623	            conn = sqlite3.connect(store.db_path)
  2624	            try:
  2625	                conn.execute("CREATE TABLE meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL)")
  2626	                conn.execute("INSERT INTO meta VALUES('schema_version','4')")
  2627	                conn.commit()
  2628	            finally:
  2629	                conn.close()
  2630	            env = self._cli_env(routing_root)
  2631	            result = subprocess.run(
  2632	                [sys.executable, "ai/scripts/set_agents_app.py", "--routing-report", "--project", str(project), "--json"],
  2633	                cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True, env=env,
  2634	            )
  2635	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1, result.stderr)
  2636	            envelope = json.loads(result.stdout)
  2637	            self.assertIn("ROUTING_SCHEMA_MIGRATION_REQUIRED", envelope["warnings"])

  2644	    def test_route_decide_cli_hermetic_matrix(self):
  2645	        # N04/F01/F02/F07: a fully hermetic decide->dispatched->terminal(->abandoned) cycle
  2646	        # against a temp routing root (the F07 seam), driving every role_class shape of the
  2647	        # F01 reason->exit table through the real CLI subprocess.
  2648	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  2649	            bins, log = self._probe_stubs(td)
  2650	            env=self._cli_env(Path(td)/"routing-root", bins)
  2651	
  2652	            def decide(descriptor):
  2653	                return self._cli_run(["--route-decide","-","--json"],env,json.dumps(descriptor))
  2654	
  2655	            # Writer: executable, ok=true, exit 0.
  2656	            writer=decide({"role":"implementer","task_class":"mechanical","selected_runtime":"codex"})
  2657	            self.assertEqual(writer.returncode,0,(writer.stdout,writer.stderr))
  2658	            wdata=json.loads(writer.stdout)
  2659	            self.assertTrue(wdata["ok"]); self.assertTrue(wdata["data"]["execution_enabled"])
  2660	            # ADR-0035 (AC-14): BILLING_RANK is now always additively present.
  2661	            self.assertEqual(len(wdata["data"]["reason_codes"]),1)
  2662	            self.assertTrue(wdata["data"]["reason_codes"][0].startswith("BILLING_RANK "))
  2663	            run_id=wdata["data"]["run_id"]; self.assertTrue(run_id.startswith("run1_"))
  2664	            self.assertEqual(wdata["data"]["tier"],"fast")
  2665	
  2666	            # docs-rw ("other" role_class): non-executable, empty reason_codes, still ok=true.
  2667	            other=decide({"role":"product-analyst","task_class":"documentation","selected_runtime":"claude-code"})
  2668	            self.assertEqual(other.returncode,0,(other.stdout,other.stderr))
  2669	            odata=json.loads(other.stdout)
  2670	            self.assertTrue(odata["ok"]); self.assertFalse(odata["data"]["execution_enabled"])
  2671	            # ADR-0035 (AC-14): BILLING_RANK is now always additively present.
  2672	            self.assertEqual(len(odata["data"]["reason_codes"]),1)
  2673	            self.assertTrue(odata["data"]["reason_codes"][0].startswith("BILLING_RANK "))
  2674	
  2675	            # Reviewer without review_of_run_id: unverified, ok=true, exit 0, tier still reported.
  2676	            unverified=decide({"role":"package-reviewer","task_class":"documentation","selected_runtime":"claude-code"})
  2677	            self.assertEqual(unverified.returncode,0,(unverified.stdout,unverified.stderr))
  2678	            udata=json.loads(unverified.stdout)
  2679	            self.assertTrue(udata["ok"]); self.assertFalse(udata["data"]["execution_enabled"])
  2680	            # ADR-0035 (AC-14): BILLING_RANK is now always additively present alongside
  2681	            # REVIEW_IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED.
  2682	            self.assertEqual(udata["data"]["reason_codes"][0],"REVIEW_IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED")
  2683	            self.assertTrue(udata["data"]["reason_codes"][1].startswith("BILLING_RANK "))
  2684	            self.assertEqual(len(udata["data"]["reason_codes"]),2)
  2685	            self.assertIsNotNone(udata["data"]["model"])
  2686	            self.assertFalse(udata["data"]["independence_verified"])
  2687	
  2688	            # An unknown role: ok=false, exit 1 — the "other side" of the F01 table.
  2689	            unknown=decide({"role":"nonexistent-role","task_class":"documentation"})
  2690	            self.assertEqual(unknown.returncode,1,(unknown.stdout,unknown.stderr))
  2691	            self.assertFalse(json.loads(unknown.stdout)["ok"])
  2692	
  2693	            # F07/lifecycle: dispatched -> terminal(success), all via CLI against the temp root.
  2694	            dispatched=self._cli_run(["--route-dispatched",run_id,"--json"],env)
  2695	            self.assertEqual(dispatched.returncode,0,(dispatched.stdout,dispatched.stderr))
  5000	
  5001	            # role_class == "other" ("decision" bias class): no pre-sort consultation.
  5002	            spied.route(routing.TaskRequest("orchestrator", "change", "documentation", selected_runtime="codex"),
  5003	                        self.observed(spied, "orchestrator", "codex"))
  5004	            spy_store.implementation_identity.assert_not_called()

  5013	    def test_service_level_role_override_wiring_reorders_via_for_tests(self):
  5014	        # RF14-04: `role_override` (not just `preference`) wired end-to-end through

  5029	    def test_model_preference_production_plumbing_end_to_end_via_real_cli(self):
  5030	        # RF14-03: proves the REAL production wiring -- `cmd_model_preference_set`'s write,
  5031	        # `load_model_preference`, `_config_with_model_preference`,
  5032	        # `config["_model_preference"]`, `RoutingService.__init__` -- actually flips the
  5033	        # live-probed provider selection end to end, never the hermetic `_for_tests` seam
  5034	        # alone (already covered above). Self-adapting to whichever two providers this
  5035	        # machine actually has authenticated, per AC-01(i)'s own "exactly one
  5036	        # independence-eligible provider on a two-provider catalog" precedent.
  5037	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  5038	            env = dict(os.environ)
  5039	            env["SET_AGENTS_ROUTING_TEST_ROOT"] = str(Path(td) / "routing")
  5040	            env["SET_AGENTS_STATE"] = str(Path(td) / "state")
  5041	            descriptor = json.dumps({"role": "product-analyst", "task_class": "documentation", "selected_runtime": "codex"})
  5042	
  5043	            def decide():
  5044	                result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "ai/scripts/set_agents_app.py", "--route-decide", "-", "--json"],
  5045	                                        cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True, env=env, input=descriptor)
  5046	                return json.loads(result.stdout)
  5047	
  5048	            baseline = decide()
  5049	            baseline_provider = baseline["data"]["provider"]
  5050	            self.assertFalse(baseline["data"]["preference_configured"])
  5051	            self.assertIsNotNone(baseline_provider, baseline)
  5052	            other = "anthropic" if baseline_provider != "anthropic" else "openai-codex"
  5053	
  5054	            setter = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "ai/scripts/set_agents_app.py",
  5055	                                     "--model-preference-set", "decision", "--provider", other, "--provider", baseline_provider],
  5056	                                    cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True, env=env)
  5057	            self.assertEqual(setter.returncode, 0, setter.stderr)
  5058	
  5059	            biased = decide()
  5060	            self.assertTrue(biased["data"]["preference_configured"], biased)
  5061	            self.assertEqual(biased["data"]["provider"], other, biased)
  5062	            self.assertNotEqual(biased["data"]["provider"], baseline_provider)

  5074	        self.assertEqual(resolve_bias_class("app-runner", {"capability": "run-ro", "duty": "ops"}), "unscoped")
  5075	        # Override precedence: role_override wins over the default predicate.

   204	        svc=self.service(simulate=True)
   205	        # A low-risk request cannot downgrade an observed high risk: the combined
   206	        # risk stays high, so missing critical coverage excludes every route.
   207	        request=routing.TaskRequest("product-analyst","change","documentation",risk="low",selected_runtime="claude-code")
   208	        facts=self.observed(svc,risk="high",context_required=False,context_present=False,critical_coverage=False)
   209	        decision=svc.route(request,facts)
   210	        self.assertFalse(decision.execution_enabled)
   211	        self.assertIn("NO_ELIGIBLE_ROUTE",decision.reason_codes)
   212	        # v2 catalog: openai rows are runtime-unavailable for claude-code, unauthenticated anthropic tiers
   213	        # fall to auth, and the eligible frontier route is excluded for context — not risk passthrough.
   214	        self.assertEqual({item["reason"] for item in decision.exclusions},
   215	                         {"RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE","PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED","CONTEXT_MISSING"})
   216	        # The same observation with low risk routes normally.
   217	        ok=svc.route(routing.TaskRequest("product-analyst","change","documentation",risk="low",selected_runtime="claude-code"),
   218	                     self.observed(svc,risk="low",context_required=False,context_present=False,critical_coverage=False))
   219	        self.assertIsNotNone(ok.route_id)
   220	        # Values outside the closed vocabularies are FACTS_INCOMPLETE, request and facts alike.
   221	        bad=svc.route(routing.TaskRequest("product-analyst","change","documentation",risk="extreme",selected_runtime="claude-code"),self.observed(svc))
   222	        self.assertEqual(bad.reason_codes,("FACTS_INCOMPLETE",))
   223	        bad=svc.route(routing.TaskRequest("product-analyst","change","documentation",selected_runtime="claude-code"),self.observed(svc,risk="extreme"))
   224	        self.assertEqual(bad.reason_codes,("FACTS_INCOMPLETE",))

   226	    def test_pi_is_pair_scoped_and_fails_closed_without_a_probed_pair(self):
   227	        # T-305: pi is now in the audited pair table (RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE no longer fires
   228	        # for it, since no route.v1.toml row narrows `runtimes`), so an unprobed pi pair
   229	        # fails closed via the SAME PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED path every other runtime uses
   230	        # — never RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE, and never vacuously executable.
   231	        # AC-01 (015): `self.inventory`'s own DEFAULT authenticates ("claude-code","anthropic")
   232	        # -- since AC-01, that is a real redirect TARGET for an anthropic route requested on
   233	        # ANY lane, "pi" included, so the default fixture would no longer prove "vacuously
   234	        # inexecutable" here. A custom inventory with NO anthropic entry anywhere is used for
   235	        # every pi-lane assertion below so this test keeps proving what it always meant to: an
   236	        # unprobed PAIR fails closed on its own, independent of a redirect existing at all --
   237	        # the redirect-interaction itself is covered by AC-01's own shape (c)/(d) tests.
   238	        # NOTE: `self.service`'s own helper does `inventory or self.inventory` -- an EMPTY
   239	        # dict is falsy and would silently fall back to the generous default fixture, so
   240	        # this is a non-empty, still anthropic-free dict, deliberately.
   241	        no_anthropic_anywhere = {("codex", "openai-codex"): {"gpt-5.6-sol"}}
   242	        svc=self.service(simulate=True, inventory=no_anthropic_anywhere); d=svc.route(routing.TaskRequest("product-analyst","change","documentation",selected_runtime="pi"),self.observed(svc,runtime="pi"))
   243	        self.assertFalse(d.execution_enabled); self.assertIn("NO_ELIGIBLE_ROUTE",d.reason_codes)
   244	        self.assertIsNone(d.route_id)  # N08: not vacuously true under simulate=True — no candidate at all
   245	        self.assertTrue(d.exclusions); self.assertTrue(all(item["reason"]=="PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED" for item in d.exclusions))
   246	        self.assertFalse(any(item["reason"]=="RUNTIME_UNAVAILABLE" for item in d.exclusions))
   247	        unavailable=self.service(simulate=True,inventory={("codex","openai-codex"):{"gpt-5.6-sol"}})
   248	        d=unavailable.route(routing.TaskRequest("product-analyst","change","documentation",selected_runtime="opencode"),self.observed(unavailable,runtime="opencode"))
   249	        self.assertFalse(d.execution_enabled); self.assertIsNone(d.route_id)
   250	        self.assertEqual(d.reason_codes,("NO_ELIGIBLE_ROUTE",))
   251	        # A real (non-simulate, writer-role) authorization attempt over an unprobed pi pair
   252	        # is excluded the same way — no durable authorization without a fresh positive probe.
   253	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   254	            real=self.service(Path(td)/"state", inventory=no_anthropic_anywhere)
   255	            d=real.route(routing.TaskRequest("implementer","change","documentation",selected_runtime="pi"),
   256	                         self.observed(real,"implementer","pi"))
   257	            self.assertFalse(d.execution_enabled); self.assertIsNone(d.route_id)
   258	            self.assertEqual(d.reason_codes,("NO_ELIGIBLE_ROUTE",))
   259	            self.assertTrue(all(item["reason"]=="PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED" for item in d.exclusions))
   260	            self.assertEqual(real.store.open_runs(),[])  # never durably authorized

   245	        provider", regardless of what the live probe would otherwise show.
   246	      - key present as a non-empty list => `("curated", set-of-ids)` -- the audited
   247	        ceiling every model must be a member of (today's only behavior, unchanged).
   248	
   249	    Same five-site lockstep `_configured_models` used to document, site (2) of 5: (1)
   250	    `models.toml`'s `[catalog]` table itself; (2) this key map (`_CATALOG_KEYS` above); (3)
   251	    `build_snapshot`'s own `configured_models` comprehension (below); (4)
   252	    `models_config.load_config`'s optional-key validation loop for the same two TOML keys
   253	    (accepts `[]` now, AC-04); (5) `models_config.emit`'s preservation loop for the same
   254	    two keys (round-trips absence AND `[]`, never inventing either).
   255	
   256	    Per ADR-0042 (022 PKG-2, decision recorded by Federico 2026-08-12, spec AC-04): in
   257	    PRACTICE only `opencode-zen`/`opencode-go` ever reach the "auto"/"veto" branches --
   258	    `models_config.load_config` dies if `[catalog].claude`/`.codex` are absent or empty, so
   259	    `openai-codex`/`anthropic` are structurally guaranteed "curated" by the time any caller
   260	    here has a validated config. `resolve_ceiling` itself stays provider-agnostic and
   261	    enforces nothing about WHICH providers may reach which state -- that restriction lives
   262	    at the validation layer (`models_config.py`) on purpose, same split ADR-0042 already
   263	    documents for the rest of this registry (billing-kind logic never bleeding into
   264	    provider identity; here, "which providers get tri-state" never bleeding into "what a
   265	    given TOML shape means").
   266	    """
   267	    key = _CATALOG_KEYS.get(provider)
   268	    if key is None or key not in config.get("catalog", {}):
   269	        return ("auto", None)
   270	    models = config["catalog"][key]
   271	    if not isinstance(models, (list, tuple)):
   272	        return ("auto", None)  # defensive only -- load_config already dies on this shape
   273	    cleaned = {item for item in models if isinstance(item, str) and item}
   274	    if not cleaned:
   275	        return ("veto", set())
   276	    return ("curated", cleaned)

   279	def _clean_lines(text: str) -> list[str]:
   280	    return [line.strip() for line in _ANSI.sub("", text).splitlines() if line.strip()]

   283	def _parse_codex_login(stdout: str, stderr: str = "") -> bool:
   284	    """`codex login status` prints a plain-text state line (observed on stderr), never JSON."""
   285	    lines = _clean_lines(stdout) or _clean_lines(stderr)
   286	    return bool(lines) and lines[0].startswith("Logged in")

   289	def _parse_claude_auth(stdout: str) -> bool:
   290	    """`claude auth status --json` is a JSON object; only loggedIn is read and the raw doc is discarded."""
   291	    doc = json.loads(stdout)
   292	    return isinstance(doc, dict) and doc.get("loggedIn") is True
   293	
   294	
   295	def _parse_opencode_auth(stdout: str) -> set[str]:
   296	    """`opencode auth list --pure` prints decorated credential rows, one provider name per
   297	    bullet. ADR-0034 (AC-07): only `●`/`*` mark a REAL, usable credential; `○` is opencode's
   298	    own "pending/incomplete" marker (e.g. an invitation not yet accepted, a session that
   299	    needs re-auth) and must never count as authenticated -- treating it as live would widen
   300	    the audited set past what is actually usable, the opposite of fail-closed. Live-measured
   301	    2026-08-10 (opencode 1.18.14): every row on this machine is `●`, so this fix has no
   302	    observable effect on today's real inventory -- it closes the defect regardless."""
   303	    providers = set()
   304	    for line in _clean_lines(stdout):
   305	        if line.startswith("Error"):
   306	            raise RoutingError("PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED")
   307	        if line.startswith(("●", "*")):
   308	            body = line.lstrip("●* ").strip()
   309	            if body:
   310	                # The trailing word is the credential method (api/oauth); the rest is the provider name.
   311	                name = body.rsplit(None, 1)[0] if len(body.split()) > 1 else body
   312	                providers.add(name.strip().lower())
   313	    return providers
   314	
   315	
   316	def _parse_opencode_models(stdout: str, provider_key: str) -> set[str]:
   317	    """`opencode models <provider> --pure` prints `<provider>/<model>` lines; errors exit 0, so text is authoritative."""
   318	    models = set()
   319	    for line in _clean_lines(stdout):
   320	        if line.startswith("Error"):
   321	            raise RoutingError("PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED")
   322	        prefix = provider_key + "/"
   323	        if not line.startswith(prefix):
   324	            raise RoutingError("PROVIDER_UNAUTHENTICATED")
   325	        model = line[len(prefix):].strip()
   326	        if model:
   327	            models.add(model)
   328	    return models
   329	
   330	
   331	def _parse_pi_models(stdout: str, provider: str) -> set[str]:
   332	    """`pi --list-models` prints a `provider  model  ...` column table (header row first,
   333	    columns whitespace-separated); only rows whose first column matches `provider` are
   334	    read. openai-codex ids are catalog-IDENTITY (returned verbatim, spike-verified);
   335	    anthropic raw ids are translated through PI_MODEL_MAP so callers intersect against the
  1840	        d2=svc.route(routing.TaskRequest("product-analyst","change","documentation",risk="high",selected_runtime="claude-code"),
  1841	                     self.observed(svc,risk="low"))
  1842	        self.assertIsNotNone(d2.route_id); self.assertEqual(tier_of(d2),"frontier")
  1843

  1863	    def _probe_stubs(self, td):
  1864	        bins=Path(td)/"bin"; bins.mkdir(); log=Path(td)/"probes.log"
  1865	        scripts={
  1866	            "codex": '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho "Logged in using ChatGPT" 1>&2\n' % log,
  1867	            "claude": '#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\necho \'{"loggedIn": true}\'\n' % log,
  1868	            "opencode": ('#!/bin/sh\necho "$0 $@" >> %s\n'
  1869	                         'if [ "$1" = "auth" ]; then printf "\\342\\227\\217  OpenAI oauth\\n"; exit 0; fi\n'
  1870	                         'if [ "$2" = "openai" ]; then echo "openai/gpt-5.6-sol"; exit 0; fi\n'
  1871	                         'echo "Error: Provider not found: $2"; exit 0\n') % log,
  1872	        }
  1873	        for name, body in scripts.items():
  1874	            path=bins/name; path.write_text(body); path.chmod(0o755)
  1875	        return bins, log
  1876	
  1877	    def test_probe_cache_is_filtering_only_redacted_and_invalidated(self):
  1878	        from routing_core import catalog as cat
  1879	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  1880	            bins, log = self._probe_stubs(td)
  1881	            cache_root=Path(td)/"root"; cache_root.mkdir(mode=0o700)
  1882	            env_patch={"PATH": f"{bins}:{os.environ['PATH']}"}
  1883	            old=os.environ["PATH"]; os.environ["PATH"]=env_patch["PATH"]
  1884	            try:
  1885	                cold=cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=cache_root, now=1000.0)
  1886	                self.assertEqual(cold[("codex","openai-codex")], set(self.config["catalog"]["codex"]))
  1887	                self.assertEqual(cold[("opencode","openai-codex")],{"gpt-5.6-sol"})
  1888	                self.assertNotIn(("opencode","anthropic"),cold)  # Error: with exit 0 fails the pair
  1889	                self.assertTrue(log.read_text())
  1890	                cache=cache_root/"probe-cache.json"
  1891	                self.assertEqual(cache.stat().st_mode & 0o777, 0o600)
  1892	                doc=json.loads(cache.read_text())
  1893	                self.assertEqual(set(doc),{"key","at","pairs"})  # redacted pair->models only
  1894	                # Warm within TTL: the two POSITIVE pairs are never re-probed, but the one
  1895	                # NEGATIVE pair (F06b: never cached, so it costs a retry every call, not the
  1896	                # whole TTL) is retried — its stub is deterministic, so the result is unchanged.
  1897	                log.write_text("")
  1898	                warm=cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=cache_root, now=1100.0)
  1899	                self.assertEqual(warm,cold)
  1900	                retried=log.read_text()
  1901	                self.assertIn("bin/opencode",retried)
  1902	                self.assertNotIn("bin/codex",retried); self.assertNotIn("bin/claude",retried)
  1903	                # Expired TTL reprobes; corrupt cache is ignored fail-closed; digest change invalidates.
  1904	                for breaker in (lambda: cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=cache_root, now=1000.0+9999),
  1905	                                lambda: (cache.write_text("{corrupt"), cat.probe_inventory(self.config, cache_root=cache_root, now=1100.0))[-1]):

  2264	    def test_report_tokens_counts_usage_status_so_mass_discard_is_visible(self):
  2265	        """F-PR-03 (review panel RP-01, upheld by finding-verifier): `usage_status`
  2266	        records a discard, but before this fix nothing aggregated it -- if Pi ever
  2267	        reported a shape that trips the `totalTokens` mismatch, every affected run would
  2268	        silently look identical to zero pi activity (NULL tokens, no count anywhere).
  2269	        """
  2270	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  2271	            svc=self.service(Path(td)/"s")
  2272	            ok_run=self.authorize(svc); svc.store.mark_dispatched(ok_run.run_id)
  2273	            svc.store.close_run(ok_run.run_id,"success",usage={"input":7,"cost":{"total":decimal.Decimal("0.001")}})
  2274	            invalid_run=self.authorize(svc); svc.store.mark_dispatched(invalid_run.run_id)
  2275	            svc.store.close_run(invalid_run.run_id,"success",usage={"input":-1})
  2276	            absent_run=self.authorize(svc)
  2277	            svc.store.close_run(absent_run.run_id,"failure")  # never dispatched -> absent
  2278	            counts=svc.store.report()["tokens"]["status_counts"]
  2279	            self.assertEqual(counts,{"ok":1,"invalid":1,"absent":1})

  2292	    def test_routing_migrate_uses_harness_identity_and_test_store(self):
  2293	        """The explicit schema-4 migration is testable and backfills the harness key.
  2294	
  2295	        The frozen DDL moved to `frozen_dispatches_script()` so 007-P1's comment- and
  2296	        CHECK-divergent fixtures come from one honest source.  With the default knobs the
  2297	        generator is byte-identical to the literal that used to sit here, and this test --
  2298	        untouched otherwise -- is what proves the extraction did not change the artifact.
  2299	        """
  2300	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
  2301	            root = Path(td) / "routing-root"
  2302	            store = routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(root)
  2303	            build_schema_db(store, frozen_dispatches_script(), schema_version=4, rows=(FROZEN_V4_ROW,))
  2304	            identity = json.loads((ROOT / "ai/state/project.json").read_text())["project_key"]
  2305	            result = self._cli_run(["--routing-migrate"], self._cli_env(root))
  2306	            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
  2307	            self.assertRegex(result.stdout, r"ROUTING_MIGRATE_OK from=4 to=7 rows=1 backup=.+")
  2308	            migrated = sqlite3.connect(f"file:{store.db_path}?mode=ro", uri=True)
  2309	            try:
  2310	                self.assertEqual(dict(migrated.execute("SELECT key,value FROM meta"))["schema_version"], "7")
  2311	                self.assertEqual(migrated.execute("SELECT project_key FROM dispatches").fetchone(), (identity,))
  2312	            finally:
  2313	                migrated.close()
  2314	            self.assertEqual(len(list((root / "backups").glob("routing-v4-*.db"))), 1)
  2315	            routing.RoutingStore._for_tests(root, project_key=identity)._validate_existing_readonly()

+# ADR-0042 (022 PKG-1/PKG-2): provider -> `[catalog]` TOML key, derived from `PROVIDERS`
+# (same values as the pre-ADR-0042 inline literal `_configured_models` used to carry). Site

+# `models_config.py` sites) are a DIFFERENT, wider concern (012 AC-04: whether a TOML key
+# exists and round-trips through `load_config`/`emit` at all) and are out of this package's
+# scope -- only this key map derives from the single provider registry.
+_CATALOG_KEYS = {provider: spec.catalog_key for provider, spec in PROVIDERS.items()}

+def resolve_ceiling(config: dict, provider: str) -> tuple[str, set[str] | None]:
+    """ADR-0042 (022 PKG-2): the tri-state ceiling every one of this file's three
+    consumers (`_probe_pairs`, `_read_probe_cache`, `build_snapshot`) reads through now,
+    replacing the two-state `_configured_models` this function inlined and removed. That
+    function only ever returned a bare `set()`, which collapsed two genuinely different
+    TOML shapes into the identical value: "no `[catalog]` key at all for this provider"
+    and "curated as an explicit empty allowlist". The FIRST must mean "no ceiling, the
+    live probe result is the routable set"; the SECOND must mean "never route this
+    provider, no matter what the probe shows" -- conflating them is exactly why
+    `_probe_pairs`'s `if not allowed: continue` skipped a provider ENTIRELY the moment its
+    `[catalog]` key was absent, which in turn forced editing `models.toml` to add any new
+    provider (the defect this whole package exists to remove).
+
+    Three states, a pure reflection of the literal TOML shape under `[catalog].<key>`:
+      - key absent from `[catalog]` entirely (or `provider` has no dedicated key at all,
+        e.g. a future `PROVIDERS` entry added without one) => `("auto", None)` -- no
+        ceiling; whatever the live probe reports for this provider IS the routable set,
+        unfiltered by any curated allowlist.
+      - key present as `[]` => `("veto", set())` -- curated, explicit "never route this
+        provider", regardless of what the live probe would otherwise show.
+      - key present as a non-empty list => `("curated", set-of-ids)` -- the audited
+        ceiling every model must be a member of (today's only behavior, unchanged).
+
+    Same five-site lockstep `_configured_models` used to document, site (2) of 5: (1)
+    `models.toml`'s `[catalog]` table itself; (2) this key map (`_CATALOG_KEYS` above); (3)
+    `build_snapshot`'s own `configured_models` comprehension (below); (4)
+    `models_config.load_config`'s optional-key validation loop for the same two TOML keys
+    (accepts `[]` now, AC-04); (5) `models_config.emit`'s preservation loop for the same
+    two keys (round-trips absence AND `[]`, never inventing either).
+
+    Per ADR-0042 (022 PKG-2, decision recorded by Federico 2026-08-12, spec AC-04): in
+    PRACTICE only `opencode-zen`/`opencode-go` ever reach the "auto"/"veto" branches --
+    `models_config.load_config` dies if `[catalog].claude`/`.codex` are absent or empty, so
+    `openai-codex`/`anthropic` are structurally guaranteed "curated" by the time any caller
+    here has a validated config. `resolve_ceiling` itself stays provider-agnostic and
+    enforces nothing about WHICH providers may reach which state -- that restriction lives
+    at the validation layer (`models_config.py`) on purpose, same split ADR-0042 already
+    documents for the rest of this registry (billing-kind logic never bleeding into
+    provider identity; here, "which providers get tri-state" never bleeding into "what a
+    given TOML shape means").
+    """
+    key = _CATALOG_KEYS.get(provider)
+    if key is None or key not in config.get("catalog", {}):
+        return ("auto", None)
+    models = config["catalog"][key]
     if not isinstance(models, (list, tuple)):
-        return set()
-    return {item for item in models if isinstance(item, str) and item}
+        return ("auto", None)  # defensive only -- load_config already dies on this shape
+    cleaned = {item for item in models if isinstance(item, str) and item}
+    if not cleaned:
+        return ("veto", set())
+    return ("curated", cleaned)
